# Plan: Agent Implementation Guide

## Context

Phases 1-4 of the agent interface refactor are merged (#360, #399). The CLI now has a clean `Agent` interface with a generic lifecycle dispatcher. Phase 5 calls for documentation. The primary audience is **AI coding agents** implementing new agent integrations, not human developers reading casually. The doc should be a self-contained, step-by-step reference with complete code templates.

## What to create

**Single file**: `docs/architecture/agent-guide.md`

Skip legacy methods entirely — focus on the new interface surface.

## Document structure

1. **Architecture overview** — 1 paragraph on the passivation pattern (agents provide data, framework orchestrates). Include the flow diagram: `Agent hook → ParseHookEvent → Event → DispatchLifecycleEvent → framework actions`.

2. **Quick reference** — Table of all interfaces (core + optional), which methods each has, and when to implement each.

3. **Step-by-step implementation guide**:
   - Step 1: Create package (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/<name>/`)
   - Step 2: Define types (hook input structs, transcript format types, tool constants)
   - Step 3: Implement core Agent interface (10 methods across 3 groups)
   - Step 4: Implement `ParseHookEvent` — the main contribution surface
   - Step 5: Choose and implement optional interfaces (decision tree)
   - Step 6: Register via `init()`
   - Step 7: Add compile-time interface assertions
   - Step 8: Implement hook installation (if HookSupport)
   - Step 9: Write tests

4. **Complete code template** — A full, copy-paste-ready skeleton for a new agent with all core methods stubbed out and annotated.

5. **Event mapping reference** — Table of all 7 event types, what they mean, what the framework does for each, and examples from both Claude Code and Gemini CLI.

6. **Optional interface decision tree** — For each optional interface: what it enables, what the framework does without it (degradation behavior), and when to implement it.

7. **Transcript format guide** — How to implement transcript storage/analysis for common formats (JSONL, JSON, custom). Reference chunking helpers.

8. **Hook installation patterns** — How to write `InstallHooks` for JSON config files (Claude pattern) and other formats.

9. **Testing patterns** — How to write unit tests for ParseHookEvent, transcript analysis, and hook installation. Reference existing test files.

10. **Common pitfalls** — Extracted from the codebase: go-git bugs, repo root vs cwd, transcript flush timing, nil event return pattern.

## Key source files to reference

- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go` — Core + optional interfaces
- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/event.go` — Event types, Event struct, ReadAndParseHookInput helper
- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/registry.go` — Registration pattern
- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/chunking.go` — Transcript chunking helpers (ChunkJSONL, ReassembleJSONL)
- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/types.go` — TokenUsage, HookInput (legacy)
- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/` — Reference implementation (full-featured)
- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/` — Reference implementation (simpler, no subagents)
- `cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go` — Dispatcher (what framework does with events)
- `cmd/entire/cli/hook_registry.go` — How hooks are wired to commands

## Verification

- Review that all interface methods are documented with correct signatures
- Cross-check against `agent.go` for completeness
- Ensure code template compiles (signatures match interfaces)
- Check that event mapping table covers all 7 event types

     1→// Package agent provides interfaces and types for integrating with coding agents.
     2→// It abstracts agent-specific behavior (hooks, log parsing, session storage) so that
     3→// the same Strategy implementations can work with any coding agent.
     4→package agent
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"io"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// Agent defines the interface for interacting with a coding agent.
    11→// Each agent implementation (Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, etc.) converts its
    12→// native format to the normalized types defined in this package.
    13→//
    14→// The interface is organized into four groups:
    15→//
    16→//   - Identity (5 methods): Name, Type, Description, DetectPresence, ProtectedDirs
    17→//   - Event Mapping (2 methods): HookNames, ParseHookEvent
    18→//   - Transcript Storage (3 methods): ReadTranscript, ChunkTranscript, ReassembleTranscript
    19→//   - Legacy (8 methods): Will be moved to optional interfaces or removed in a future phase
    20→type Agent interface {
    21→	// --- Identity ---
    22→
    23→	// Name returns the agent registry key (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini")
    24→	Name() AgentName
    25→
    26→	// Type returns the agent type identifier (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI")
    27→	// This is stored in metadata and trailers.
    28→	Type() AgentType
    29→
    30→	// Description returns a human-readable description for UI
    31→	Description() string
    32→
    33→	// DetectPresence checks if this agent is configured in the repository
    34→	DetectPresence() (bool, error)
    35→
    36→	// ProtectedDirs returns repo-root-relative directories that should never be
    37→	// modified or deleted during rewind or other destructive operations.
    38→	// Examples: [".claude"] for Claude, [".gemini"] for Gemini.
    39→	ProtectedDirs() []string
    40→
    41→	// --- Event Mapping ---
    42→
    43→	// HookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
    44→	// These become subcommands under `entire hooks <agent>`.
    45→	// e.g., ["stop", "user-prompt-submit", "session-start", "session-end"]
    46→	HookNames() []string
    47→
    48→	// ParseHookEvent translates an agent-native hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    49→	// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    50→	// This is the core contribution surface for new agent implementations.
    51→	ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)
    52→
    53→	// --- Transcript Storage ---
    54→
    55→	// ReadTranscript reads the raw transcript bytes for a session.
    56→	ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)
    57→
    58→	// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks if it exceeds maxSize.
    59→	// Returns a slice of chunks. If the transcript fits in one chunk, returns single-element slice.
    60→	// The chunking is format-aware: JSONL splits at line boundaries, JSON splits message arrays.
    61→	ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
    62→
    63→	// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.
    64→	// Handles format-specific reassembly (JSONL concatenation, JSON message merging).
    65→	ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
    66→
    67→	// --- Legacy methods (will move to optional interfaces in Phase 4) ---
    68→
    69→	// GetHookConfigPath returns path to hook config file (empty if none).
    70→	GetHookConfigPath() string
    71→
    72→	// SupportsHooks returns true if agent supports lifecycle hooks.
    73→	SupportsHooks() bool
    74→
    75→	// ParseHookInput parses hook callback input from stdin.
    76→	ParseHookInput(hookType HookType, reader io.Reader) (*HookInput, error)
    77→
    78→	// GetSessionID extracts session ID from hook input.
    79→	GetSessionID(input *HookInput) string
    80→
    81→	// GetSessionDir returns where agent stores session data for this repo.
    82→	GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)
    83→
    84→	// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to the session transcript file.
    85→	ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string
    86→
    87→	// ReadSession reads session data from agent's storage.
    88→	ReadSession(input *HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)
    89→
    90→	// WriteSession writes session data for resumption.
    91→	WriteSession(session *AgentSession) error
    92→
    93→	// FormatResumeCommand returns command to resume a session.
    94→	FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string
    95→}
    96→
    97→// HookSupport is implemented by agents with lifecycle hooks.
    98→// This optional interface allows agents like Claude Code and Cursor to
    99→// install and manage hooks that notify Entire of agent events.
   100→type HookSupport interface {
   101→	Agent
   102→
   103→	// InstallHooks installs agent-specific hooks.
   104→	// If localDev is true, hooks point to local development build.
   105→	// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
   106→	// Returns the number of hooks installed.
   107→	InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error)
   108→
   109→	// UninstallHooks removes installed hooks
   110→	UninstallHooks() error
   111→
   112→	// AreHooksInstalled checks if hooks are currently installed
   113→	AreHooksInstalled() bool
   114→
   115→	// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types this agent supports
   116→	GetSupportedHooks() []HookType
   117→}
   118→
   119→// HookHandler is implemented by agents that define their own hook vocabulary.
   120→// HookNames() is now part of the core Agent interface.
   121→// This interface is kept for backward compatibility during migration.
   122→type HookHandler interface {
   123→	Agent
   124→
   125→	// GetHookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
   126→	GetHookNames() []string
   127→}
   128→
   129→// FileWatcher is implemented by agents that use file-based detection.
   130→// Agents like Aider that don't support hooks can use file watching
   131→// to detect session activity.
   132→type FileWatcher interface {
   133→	Agent
   134→
   135→	// GetWatchPaths returns paths to watch for session changes
   136→	GetWatchPaths() ([]string, error)
   137→
   138→	// OnFileChange handles a detected file change and returns session info
   139→	OnFileChange(path string) (*SessionChange, error)
   140→}
   141→
   142→// TranscriptAnalyzer provides format-specific transcript parsing.
   143→// Agents that implement this get richer checkpoints (transcript-derived file lists,
   144→// prompts, summaries). Agents that don't still participate in the checkpoint lifecycle
   145→// via git-status-based file detection and raw transcript storage.
   146→type TranscriptAnalyzer interface {
   147→	Agent
   148→
   149→	// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current position (length) of a transcript.
   150→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), this is the line count.
   151→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), this is the message count.
   152→	// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   153→	GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error)
   154→
   155→	// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given offset.
   156→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), offset is the starting line number.
   157→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), offset is the starting message index.
   158→	// Returns:
   159→	//   - files: list of file paths modified by the agent (from Write/Edit tools)
   160→	//   - currentPosition: the current position (line count or message count)
   161→	//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   162→	ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error)
   163→
   164→	// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given offset.
   165→	ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error)
   166→
   167→	// ExtractSummary extracts a summary of the session from the transcript.
   168→	ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error)
   169→}
   170→
   171→// TranscriptPreparer is called before ReadTranscript to handle agent-specific
   172→// flush/sync requirements (e.g., Claude Code's async transcript writing).
   173→// The framework calls PrepareTranscript before ReadTranscript if implemented.
   174→type TranscriptPreparer interface {
   175→	Agent
   176→
   177→	// PrepareTranscript ensures the transcript is ready to read.
   178→	// For Claude Code, this waits for the async transcript flush to complete.
   179→	PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error
   180→}
   181→
   182→// TokenCalculator provides token usage calculation for a session.
   183→// The framework calls this during step save and checkpoint if implemented.
   184→type TokenCalculator interface {
   185→	Agent
   186→
   187→	// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given offset.
   188→	CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*TokenUsage, error)
   189→}
   190→
   191→// SubagentAwareExtractor provides methods for extracting files and tokens including subagents.
   192→// Agents that support spawning subagents (like Claude Code's Task tool) should implement this
   193→// to ensure subagent contributions are included in checkpoints.
   194→type SubagentAwareExtractor interface {
   195→	Agent
   196→
   197→	// ExtractAllModifiedFiles extracts files modified by both the main agent and any spawned subagents.
   198→	// The subagentsDir parameter specifies where subagent transcripts are stored.
   199→	// Returns a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
   200→	ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error)
   201→
   202→	// CalculateTotalTokenUsage computes token usage including all spawned subagents.
   203→	// The subagentsDir parameter specifies where subagent transcripts are stored.
   204→	CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) (*TokenUsage, error)
   205→}

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"errors"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"io"
     8→	"time"
     9→)
    10→
    11→// EventType represents a normalized lifecycle event from any agent.
    12→// Agents translate their native hooks into these event types via ParseHookEvent.
    13→type EventType int
    14→
    15→const (
    16→	// SessionStart indicates the agent session has begun.
    17→	SessionStart EventType = iota + 1
    18→
    19→	// TurnStart indicates the user submitted a prompt and the agent is about to work.
    20→	TurnStart
    21→
    22→	// TurnEnd indicates the agent finished responding to a prompt.
    23→	TurnEnd
    24→
    25→	// Compaction indicates the agent is about to compress its context window.
    26→	// This triggers the same save logic as TurnEnd but also resets the transcript offset.
    27→	Compaction
    28→
    29→	// SessionEnd indicates the session has been terminated.
    30→	SessionEnd
    31→
    32→	// SubagentStart indicates a subagent (task) has been spawned.
    33→	SubagentStart
    34→
    35→	// SubagentEnd indicates a subagent (task) has completed.
    36→	SubagentEnd
    37→)
    38→
    39→// String returns a human-readable name for the event type.
    40→func (e EventType) String() string {
    41→	switch e {
    42→	case SessionStart:
    43→		return "SessionStart"
    44→	case TurnStart:
    45→		return "TurnStart"
    46→	case TurnEnd:
    47→		return "TurnEnd"
    48→	case Compaction:
    49→		return "Compaction"
    50→	case SessionEnd:
    51→		return "SessionEnd"
    52→	case SubagentStart:
    53→		return "SubagentStart"
    54→	case SubagentEnd:
    55→		return "SubagentEnd"
    56→	default:
    57→		return "Unknown"
    58→	}
    59→}
    60→
    61→// Event is a normalized lifecycle event produced by an agent's ParseHookEvent method.
    62→// The framework dispatcher uses these events to drive checkpoint/session lifecycle actions.
    63→type Event struct {
    64→	// Type is the kind of lifecycle event.
    65→	Type EventType
    66→
    67→	// SessionID identifies the agent session.
    68→	SessionID string
    69→
    70→	// PreviousSessionID is non-empty when this event represents a session continuation
    71→	// or handoff (e.g., Claude starting a new session ID after exiting plan mode).
    72→	PreviousSessionID string
    73→
    74→	// SessionRef is an agent-specific reference to the transcript (typically a file path).
    75→	SessionRef string
    76→
    77→	// Prompt is the user's prompt text (populated on TurnStart events).
    78→	Prompt string
    79→
    80→	// Timestamp is when the event occurred.
    81→	Timestamp time.Time
    82→
    83→	// ToolUseID identifies the tool invocation (for SubagentStart/SubagentEnd events).
    84→	ToolUseID string
    85→
    86→	// SubagentID identifies the subagent instance (for SubagentEnd events).
    87→	SubagentID string
    88→
    89→	// ToolInput is the raw tool input JSON (for subagent type/description extraction).
    90→	ToolInput json.RawMessage
    91→
    92→	// ResponseMessage is an optional message to display to the user via the agent.
    93→	ResponseMessage string
    94→
    95→	// Metadata holds agent-specific state that the framework stores and makes available
    96→	// on subsequent events. Examples: Pi's activeLeafId, Cursor's is_background_agent.
    97→	Metadata map[string]string
    98→}
    99→
   100→// ReadAndParseHookInput reads all bytes from stdin and unmarshals JSON into the given type.
   101→// This is a shared helper for agent ParseHookEvent implementations.
   102→func ReadAndParseHookInput[T any](stdin io.Reader) (*T, error) {
   103→	data, err := io.ReadAll(stdin)
   104→	if err != nil {
   105→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read hook input: %w", err)
   106→	}
   107→	if len(data) == 0 {
   108→		return nil, errors.New("empty hook input")
   109→	}
   110→	var result T
   111→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &result); err != nil {
   112→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   113→	}
   114→	return &result, nil
   115→}
   116→

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"slices"
     6→	"sync"
     7→)
     8→
     9→var (
    10→	registryMu sync.RWMutex
    11→	registry   = make(map[AgentName]Factory)
    12→)
    13→
    14→// Factory creates a new agent instance
    15→type Factory func() Agent
    16→
    17→// Register adds an agent factory to the registry.
    18→// Called from init() in each agent implementation.
    19→func Register(name AgentName, factory Factory) {
    20→	registryMu.Lock()
    21→	defer registryMu.Unlock()
    22→	registry[name] = factory
    23→}
    24→
    25→// Get retrieves an agent by name.
    26→//
    27→
    28→func Get(name AgentName) (Agent, error) {
    29→	registryMu.RLock()
    30→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    31→
    32→	factory, ok := registry[name]
    33→	if !ok {
    34→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent: %s (available: %v)", name, List())
    35→	}
    36→	return factory(), nil
    37→}
    38→
    39→// List returns all registered agent names in sorted order.
    40→func List() []AgentName {
    41→	registryMu.RLock()
    42→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    43→
    44→	names := make([]AgentName, 0, len(registry))
    45→	for name := range registry {
    46→		names = append(names, name)
    47→	}
    48→	slices.Sort(names)
    49→	return names
    50→}
    51→
    52→// DetectAll returns all agents whose DetectPresence reports true.
    53→// Agents are checked in sorted name order (via List()) for deterministic results.
    54→// Returns an empty slice when no agent is detected.
    55→func DetectAll() []Agent {
    56→	names := List() // sorted, lock-safe
    57→
    58→	var detected []Agent
    59→	for _, name := range names {
    60→		ag, err := Get(name)
    61→		if err != nil {
    62→			continue
    63→		}
    64→		if present, err := ag.DetectPresence(); err == nil && present {
    65→			detected = append(detected, ag)
    66→		}
    67→	}
    68→	return detected
    69→}
    70→
    71→// Detect attempts to auto-detect which agent is being used.
    72→// Iterates registered agents in sorted name order for deterministic results.
    73→// Returns the first agent whose DetectPresence reports true.
    74→func Detect() (Agent, error) {
    75→	detected := DetectAll()
    76→	if len(detected) == 0 {
    77→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no agent detected (available: %v)", List())
    78→	}
    79→	return detected[0], nil
    80→}
    81→
    82→// AgentName is the registry key type for agents (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini").
    83→//
    84→//nolint:revive // stuttering is intentional - distinguishes from AgentType when both are used
    85→type AgentName string
    86→
    87→// AgentType is the display name type stored in metadata/trailers (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI").
    88→//
    89→//nolint:revive // stuttering is intentional - distinguishes from AgentName when both are used
    90→type AgentType string
    91→
    92→// Agent name constants (registry keys)
    93→const (
    94→	AgentNameClaudeCode AgentName = "claude-code"
    95→	AgentNameGemini     AgentName = "gemini"
    96→)
    97→
    98→// Agent type constants (type identifiers stored in metadata/trailers)
    99→const (
   100→	AgentTypeClaudeCode AgentType = "Claude Code"
   101→	AgentTypeGemini     AgentType = "Gemini CLI"
   102→	AgentTypeUnknown    AgentType = "Agent" // Fallback for backwards compatibility
   103→)
   104→
   105→// DefaultAgentName is the registry key for the default agent.
   106→const DefaultAgentName AgentName = AgentNameClaudeCode
   107→
   108→// GetByAgentType retrieves an agent by its type identifier.
   109→//
   110→// Note: This uses a linear search that instantiates agents until a match is found.
   111→// This is acceptable because:
   112→//   - Agent count is small (~2-20 agents)
   113→//   - Agent factories are lightweight (empty struct allocation)
   114→//   - Called infrequently (commit hooks, rewind, debug commands - not hot paths)
   115→//   - Cost is ~400ns worst case vs milliseconds for I/O operations
   116→//
   117→// Only optimize if agent count exceeds 100 or profiling shows this as a bottleneck.
   118→func GetByAgentType(agentType AgentType) (Agent, error) {
   119→	registryMu.RLock()
   120→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
   121→
   122→	for _, factory := range registry {
   123→		ag := factory()
   124→		if ag.Type() == agentType {
   125→			return ag, nil
   126→		}
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent type: %s", agentType)
   130→}
   131→
   132→// AllProtectedDirs returns the union of ProtectedDirs from all registered agents.
   133→func AllProtectedDirs() []string {
   134→	// Copy factories under the lock, then release before calling external code.
   135→	registryMu.RLock()
   136→	factories := make([]Factory, 0, len(registry))
   137→	for _, f := range registry {
   138→		factories = append(factories, f)
   139→	}
   140→	registryMu.RUnlock()
   141→
   142→	seen := make(map[string]struct{})
   143→	var dirs []string
   144→	for _, factory := range factories {
   145→		for _, d := range factory().ProtectedDirs() {
   146→			if _, ok := seen[d]; !ok {
   147→				seen[d] = struct{}{}
   148→				dirs = append(dirs, d)
   149→			}
   150→		}
   151→	}
   152→	slices.Sort(dirs)
   153→	return dirs
   154→}
   155→
   156→// Default returns the default agent.
   157→// Returns nil if the default agent is not registered.
   158→//
   159→//nolint:errcheck // Factory pattern returns interface; error is acceptable to ignore for default
   160→func Default() Agent {
   161→	a, _ := Get(DefaultAgentName)
   162→	return a
   163→}
   164→

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"sort"
     7→	"strings"
     8→)
     9→
    10→const (
    11→	// MaxChunkSize is the maximum size for a single transcript chunk.
    12→	// GitHub has a 100MB limit per blob, so we use 50MB to be safe.
    13→	MaxChunkSize = 50 * 1024 * 1024 // 50MB
    14→
    15→	// ChunkSuffix is the format for chunk file suffixes (e.g., ".001", ".002")
    16→	ChunkSuffix = ".%03d"
    17→)
    18→
    19→// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks using the appropriate agent.
    20→// If agentType is empty or the agent is not found, falls back to JSONL (line-based) chunking.
    21→func ChunkTranscript(content []byte, agentType AgentType) ([][]byte, error) {
    22→	if len(content) <= MaxChunkSize {
    23→		return [][]byte{content}, nil
    24→	}
    25→
    26→	// Try to get the agent by type and use its format-aware chunking
    27→	if agentType != "" {
    28→		ag, err := GetByAgentType(agentType)
    29→		if err == nil {
    30→			chunks, chunkErr := ag.ChunkTranscript(content, MaxChunkSize)
    31→			if chunkErr != nil {
    32→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent chunking failed: %w", chunkErr)
    33→			}
    34→			return chunks, nil
    35→		}
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	// Fall back to JSONL chunking (default)
    39→	return ChunkJSONL(content, MaxChunkSize)
    40→}
    41→
    42→// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.
    43→// If agentType is empty or the agent is not found, falls back to JSONL (line-based) reassembly.
    44→func ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte, agentType AgentType) ([]byte, error) {
    45→	if len(chunks) == 0 {
    46→		return nil, nil
    47→	}
    48→	if len(chunks) == 1 {
    49→		return chunks[0], nil
    50→	}
    51→
    52→	// Try to get the agent by type and use its format-aware reassembly
    53→	if agentType != "" {
    54→		ag, err := GetByAgentType(agentType)
    55→		if err == nil {
    56→			result, reassembleErr := ag.ReassembleTranscript(chunks)
    57→			if reassembleErr != nil {
    58→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent reassembly failed: %w", reassembleErr)
    59→			}
    60→			return result, nil
    61→		}
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	// Fall back to JSONL reassembly (default)
    65→	return ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
    66→}
    67→
    68→// ChunkJSONL splits JSONL content at line boundaries.
    69→// This is the default chunking for agents using JSONL format (like Claude Code).
    70→func ChunkJSONL(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
    71→	// Handle empty content
    72→	if len(content) == 0 {
    73→		return [][]byte{}, nil
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	lines := strings.Split(string(content), "\n")
    77→	var chunks [][]byte
    78→	var currentChunk strings.Builder
    79→
    80→	for i, line := range lines {
    81→		// Check if adding this line would exceed the chunk size
    82→		lineWithNewline := line + "\n"
    83→
    84→		// Check if a single line exceeds maxSize - this is an error since we can't split JSONL lines
    85→		if len(lineWithNewline) > maxSize {
    86→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("JSONL line %d exceeds maximum chunk size (%d bytes > %d bytes); cannot split a single JSON object", i+1, len(lineWithNewline), maxSize)
    87→		}
    88→
    89→		if currentChunk.Len()+len(lineWithNewline) > maxSize && currentChunk.Len() > 0 {
    90→			// Save current chunk and start a new one
    91→			chunks = append(chunks, []byte(strings.TrimSuffix(currentChunk.String(), "\n")))
    92→			currentChunk.Reset()
    93→		}
    94→		currentChunk.WriteString(lineWithNewline)
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	// Add the last chunk if it has content
    98→	if currentChunk.Len() > 0 {
    99→		chunks = append(chunks, []byte(strings.TrimSuffix(currentChunk.String(), "\n")))
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	return chunks, nil
   103→}
   104→
   105→// ReassembleJSONL concatenates JSONL chunks with newlines.
   106→func ReassembleJSONL(chunks [][]byte) []byte {
   107→	var result strings.Builder
   108→	for i, chunk := range chunks {
   109→		result.Write(chunk)
   110→		if i < len(chunks)-1 {
   111→			result.WriteString("\n")
   112→		}
   113→	}
   114→	return []byte(result.String())
   115→}
   116→
   117→// ChunkFileName returns the filename for a chunk at the given index.
   118→// Index 0 returns the base filename, index 1+ returns with chunk suffix.
   119→func ChunkFileName(baseName string, index int) string {
   120→	if index == 0 {
   121→		return baseName
   122→	}
   123→	return baseName + fmt.Sprintf(ChunkSuffix, index)
   124→}
   125→
   126→// ParseChunkIndex extracts the chunk index from a filename.
   127→// Returns 0 for the base file (no suffix), or the chunk number for suffixed files.
   128→// Returns -1 if the filename doesn't match the expected pattern.
   129→func ParseChunkIndex(filename, baseName string) int {
   130→	if filename == baseName {
   131→		return 0
   132→	}
   133→
   134→	if !strings.HasPrefix(filename, baseName+".") {
   135→		return -1
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	suffix := strings.TrimPrefix(filename, baseName+".")
   139→	var index int
   140→	if _, err := fmt.Sscanf(suffix, "%03d", &index); err != nil {
   141→		return -1
   142→	}
   143→	return index
   144→}
   145→
   146→// SortChunkFiles sorts chunk filenames in order (base file first, then numbered chunks).
   147→func SortChunkFiles(files []string, baseName string) []string {
   148→	sorted := make([]string, len(files))
   149→	copy(sorted, files)
   150→
   151→	sort.Slice(sorted, func(i, j int) bool {
   152→		idxI := ParseChunkIndex(sorted[i], baseName)
   153→		idxJ := ParseChunkIndex(sorted[j], baseName)
   154→		return idxI < idxJ
   155→	})
   156→
   157→	return sorted
   158→}
   159→
   160→// geminiTranscriptDetect is used for detecting Gemini JSON format.
   161→type geminiTranscriptDetect struct {
   162→	Messages []interface{} `json:"messages"`
   163→}
   164→
   165→// DetectAgentTypeFromContent detects the agent type from transcript content.
   166→// Returns AgentTypeGemini if it appears to be Gemini JSON format, empty AgentType otherwise.
   167→// This is used when the agent type is unknown but we need to chunk/reassemble correctly.
   168→func DetectAgentTypeFromContent(content []byte) AgentType {
   169→	// Quick check: Gemini JSON starts with { and has a messages array
   170→	trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(string(content))
   171→	if !strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "{") {
   172→		return ""
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Try to parse as Gemini JSON format (object with messages array)
   176→	var transcript geminiTranscriptDetect
   177→	if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &transcript); err != nil {
   178→		return ""
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Must have at least one message to be considered Gemini format
   182→	if len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   183→		return AgentTypeGemini
   184→	}
   185→
   186→	return ""
   187→}
   188→

     5→// HookType represents agent lifecycle events
     6→type HookType string
     7→
     8→const (
     9→	HookSessionStart     HookType = "session_start"
    10→	HookSessionEnd       HookType = "session_end"
    11→	HookUserPromptSubmit HookType = "user_prompt_submit"
    12→	HookStop             HookType = "stop"
    13→	HookPreToolUse       HookType = "pre_tool_use"
    14→	HookPostToolUse      HookType = "post_tool_use"
    15→)

    17→// HookInput contains normalized data from hook callbacks
    18→type HookInput struct {
    19→	HookType  HookType
    20→	SessionID string
    21→	// SessionRef is an agent-specific session reference (file path, db key, etc.)
    22→	SessionRef string
    23→	Timestamp  time.Time
    24→
    25→	// UserPrompt is the user's prompt text (from UserPromptSubmit hooks)
    26→	UserPrompt string
    27→
    28→	// Tool-specific fields (PreToolUse/PostToolUse)
    29→	ToolName     string
    30→	ToolUseID    string
    31→	ToolInput    []byte // Raw JSON
    32→	ToolResponse []byte // Raw JSON (PostToolUse only)
    33→
    34→	// RawData preserves agent-specific data for extension
    35→	RawData map[string]interface{}
    36→}
    37→
    38→// SessionChange represents detected session activity (for FileWatcher)
    39→type SessionChange struct {
    40→	SessionID  string
    41→	SessionRef string
    42→	EventType  HookType
    43→	Timestamp  time.Time
    44→}
    45→
    46→// TokenUsage represents aggregated token usage for a checkpoint.
    47→// This is agent-agnostic and can be populated by any agent that tracks token usage.
    48→type TokenUsage struct {
    49→	// InputTokens is the number of input tokens (fresh, not from cache)
    50→	InputTokens int `json:"input_tokens"`
    51→	// CacheCreationTokens is tokens written to cache (billable at cache write rate)
    52→	CacheCreationTokens int `json:"cache_creation_tokens"`
    53→	// CacheReadTokens is tokens read from cache (discounted rate)
    54→	CacheReadTokens int `json:"cache_read_tokens"`
    55→	// OutputTokens is the number of output tokens generated
    56→	OutputTokens int `json:"output_tokens"`
    57→	// APICallCount is the number of API calls made
    58→	APICallCount int `json:"api_call_count"`
    59→	// SubagentTokens contains token usage from spawned subagents (if any)
    60→	SubagentTokens *TokenUsage `json:"subagent_tokens,omitempty"`
    61→}

     1→// lifecycle.go implements the generic lifecycle event dispatcher.
     2→// It routes normalized events from any agent to the appropriate framework actions.
     3→//
     4→// The dispatcher inverts the current flow from "agent handler calls framework functions"
     5→// to "framework dispatcher calls agent methods." Agents are passive data providers;
     6→// the dispatcher handles all orchestration: state transitions, strategy calls,
     7→// file change detection, metadata generation.
     8→package cli
     9→
    10→import (
    11→	"context"
    12→	"errors"
    13→	"fmt"
    14→	"log/slog"
    15→	"os"
    16→	"path/filepath"
    17→	"strings"
    18→	"time"
    19→
    20→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    21→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    22→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    23→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    24→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    25→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    26→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/validation"
    27→)
    28→
    29→// DispatchLifecycleEvent routes a normalized lifecycle event to the appropriate handler.
    30→// Returns nil if the event was handled successfully.
    31→func DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    32→	if ag == nil {
    33→		return errors.New("agent cannot be nil")
    34→	}
    35→	if event == nil {
    36→		return errors.New("event cannot be nil")
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	switch event.Type {
    40→	case agent.SessionStart:
    41→		return handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag, event)
    42→	case agent.TurnStart:
    43→		return handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag, event)
    44→	case agent.TurnEnd:
    45→		return handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event)
    46→	case agent.Compaction:
    47→		return handleLifecycleCompaction(ag, event)
    48→	case agent.SessionEnd:
    49→		return handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag, event)
    50→	case agent.SubagentStart:
    51→		return handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag, event)
    52→	case agent.SubagentEnd:
    53→		return handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag, event)
    54→	default:
    55→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown lifecycle event type: %d", event.Type)
    56→	}
    57→}
    58→
    59→// handleLifecycleSessionStart handles session start: shows banner, checks concurrent sessions,
    60→// fires state machine transition.
    61→func handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    62→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
    63→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
    64→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
    65→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
    66→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
    67→	)
    68→
    69→	if event.SessionID == "" {
    70→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
    71→	}
    72→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(event.SessionID); err != nil {
    73→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	// Build informational message
    77→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
    78→
    79→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
    80→	strat := GetStrategy()
    81→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
    82→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(event.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
    83→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Output informational message
    88→	if event.ResponseMessage != "" {
    89→		message = event.ResponseMessage
    90→	}
    91→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
    92→		return err
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
    96→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
    97→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
    98→	} else if state != nil {
    99→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   100→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   101→		}
   102→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
   103→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   104→		}
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	return nil
   108→}
   109→
   110→// handleLifecycleTurnStart handles turn start: captures pre-prompt state,
   111→// ensures strategy setup, initializes session.
   112→func handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   113→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   114→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-start",
   115→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   116→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   117→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   118→	)
   119→
   120→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   121→	if sessionID == "" {
   122→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
   123→	}
   124→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   125→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	// Capture pre-prompt state (including transcript position via TranscriptAnalyzer)
   129→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(ag, sessionID, event.SessionRef); err != nil {
   130→		return err
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	// Ensure strategy setup and initialize session
   134→	strat := GetStrategy()
   135→
   136→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   137→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   141→		agentType := ag.Type()
   142→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(sessionID, agentType, event.SessionRef, event.Prompt); err != nil {
   143→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   144→		}
   145→	}
   146→
   147→	return nil
   148→}
   149→
   150→// handleLifecycleTurnEnd handles turn end: validates transcript, extracts metadata,
   151→// detects file changes, saves step + checkpoint, transitions phase.
   152→//
   153→//nolint:maintidx // high complexity due to sequential orchestration of 8 steps (validation, extraction, file detection, filtering, token calc, step save, phase transition, cleanup) - splitting would obscure the flow
   154→func handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   155→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   156→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-end",
   157→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   158→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   159→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   160→	)
   161→
   162→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   163→	if sessionID == "" {
   164→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	transcriptRef := event.SessionRef
   168→	if transcriptRef == "" {
   169→		return errors.New("transcript file not specified")
   170→	}
   171→	if !fileExists(transcriptRef) {
   172→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found: %s", transcriptRef)
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   176→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   177→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   178→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Create session metadata directory
   182→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   183→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   184→	if err != nil {
   185→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir
   186→	}
   187→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   188→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// If agent implements TranscriptPreparer, wait for transcript to be ready
   192→	if preparer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptPreparer); ok {
   193→		if err := preparer.PrepareTranscript(transcriptRef); err != nil {
   194→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to prepare transcript: %v\n", err)
   195→		}
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Copy transcript to session directory
   199→	transcriptData, err := ag.ReadTranscript(transcriptRef)
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   204→	if err := os.WriteFile(logFile, transcriptData, 0o600); err != nil {
   205→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   208→
   209→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on TurnStart)
   210→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   211→	if err != nil {
   212→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	// Determine transcript offset
   216→	transcriptOffset := resolveTranscriptOffset(preState, sessionID)
   217→
   218→	// Extract metadata via agent interface (prompts, summary, modified files)
   219→	var allPrompts []string
   220→	var summary string
   221→	var modifiedFiles []string
   222→	var newTranscriptPosition int
   223→
   224→	// Compute subagents directory for agents that support subagent extraction.
   225→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/
   226→	subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptRef), event.SessionID, "subagents")
   227→
   228→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   229→		// Extract prompts
   230→		if prompts, promptErr := analyzer.ExtractPrompts(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); promptErr != nil {
   231→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract prompts: %v\n", promptErr)
   232→		} else {
   233→			allPrompts = prompts
   234→		}
   235→
   236→		// Extract summary
   237→		if s, sumErr := analyzer.ExtractSummary(transcriptRef); sumErr != nil {
   238→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract summary: %v\n", sumErr)
   239→		} else {
   240→			summary = s
   241→		}
   242→
   243→		// Extract modified files - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor if available to include subagent files
   244→		if subagentExtractor, subOk := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); subOk {
   245→			if files, fileErr := subagentExtractor.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset, subagentsDir); fileErr != nil {
   246→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files (with subagents): %v\n", fileErr)
   247→			} else {
   248→				modifiedFiles = files
   249→			}
   250→			// Get position from basic analyzer
   251→			if _, pos, posErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); posErr == nil {
   252→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   253→			}
   254→		} else {
   255→			// Fall back to basic extraction (main transcript only)
   256→			if files, pos, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); fileErr != nil {
   257→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files: %v\n", fileErr)
   258→			} else {
   259→				modifiedFiles = files
   260→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   261→			}
   262→		}
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	// Write prompts file
   266→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   267→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   268→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   269→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   270→	}
   271→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   272→
   273→	// Write summary file
   274→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   275→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   276→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   277→	}
   278→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   279→
   280→	// Generate commit message from last prompt
   281→	lastPrompt := ""
   282→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   283→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   284→	}
   285→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   286→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   287→
   288→	// Get repo root for path normalization
   289→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   290→	if err != nil {
   291→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   292→	}
   293→
   294→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   295→	if preState != nil {
   296→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   297→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   298→	}
   299→
   300→	// Detect file changes via git status
   301→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   302→	if err != nil {
   303→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	// Filter and normalize all paths
   307→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   308→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   309→	if changes != nil {
   310→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   311→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   312→	}
   313→
   314→	// Filter transcript-extracted files to exclude files already committed to HEAD.
   315→	// When an agent commits files mid-turn, those files are condensed by PostCommit
   316→	// and should not be re-added to FilesTouched by SaveStep. A file is "committed"
   317→	// if it exists in HEAD with the same content as the working tree.
   318→	relModifiedFiles = filterToUncommittedFiles(relModifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   319→
   320→	// Check if there are any changes
   321→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   322→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   323→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   324→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   325→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   326→		if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   327→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   328→		}
   329→		return nil
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	// Log file changes
   333→	logFileChanges(relModifiedFiles, relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles)
   334→
   335→	// Create context file
   336→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   337→	if err := createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, allPrompts, summary); err != nil {
   338→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   339→	}
   340→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   341→
   342→	// Get git author
   343→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   344→	if err != nil {
   345→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	// Get strategy and agent type
   349→	strat := GetStrategy()
   350→	agentType := ag.Type()
   351→
   352→	// Get transcript position/identifier from pre-prompt state
   353→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   354→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   355→	if preState != nil {
   356→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   357→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.TranscriptOffset
   358→	}
   359→
   360→	// Calculate token usage - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor to include subagent tokens
   361→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   362→	if subagentExtractor, ok := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); ok {
   363→		usage, tokenErr := subagentExtractor.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   364→		if tokenErr != nil {
   365→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage (with subagents): %v\n", tokenErr)
   366→		} else {
   367→			tokenUsage = usage
   368→		}
   369→	} else if calculator, ok := ag.(agent.TokenCalculator); ok {
   370→		// Fall back to basic token calculation (main transcript only)
   371→		usage, tokenErr := calculator.CalculateTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart)
   372→		if tokenErr != nil {
   373→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   374→		} else {
   375→			tokenUsage = usage
   376→		}
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	// Build fully-populated step context and delegate to strategy
   380→	ctx := strategy.StepContext{
   381→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   382→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   383→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   384→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   385→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   386→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   387→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   388→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptRef,
   389→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   390→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   391→		AgentType:                agentType,
   392→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   393→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   394→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   395→	}
   396→
   397→	if err := strat.SaveStep(ctx); err != nil {
   398→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save step: %w", err)
   399→	}
   400→
   401→	// Update session state transcript position for auto-commit strategy
   402→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && newTranscriptPosition > 0 {
   403→		updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID, newTranscriptPosition)
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	// Transition session phase and cleanup
   407→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   408→	if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   409→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   410→	}
   411→
   412→	return nil
   413→}
   414→
   415→// handleLifecycleCompaction handles context compaction: saves current progress
   416→// but stays in ACTIVE phase (unlike TurnEnd which transitions to IDLE).
   417→// Also resets the transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated.
   418→func handleLifecycleCompaction(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   419→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   420→	logging.Info(logCtx, "compaction",
   421→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   422→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   423→	)
   424→
   425→	// Fire EventCompaction to trigger ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched (stays in ACTIVE)
   426→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   427→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   428→	if loadErr != nil {
   429→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for compaction: %v\n", loadErr)
   430→	}
   431→	if sessionState != nil {
   432→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(sessionState, session.EventCompaction, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   433→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: compaction transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   434→		}
   435→
   436→		// Reset transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated/reorganized
   437→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
   438→
   439→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); saveErr != nil {
   440→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save session state after compaction: %v\n", saveErr)
   441→		}
   442→	}
   443→
   444→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Context compaction: transcript offset reset\n")
   445→	return nil
   446→}
   447→
   448→// handleLifecycleSessionEnd handles session end: marks the session as ended.
   449→func handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   450→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   451→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   452→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   453→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   454→	)
   455→
   456→	if event.SessionID == "" {
   457→		return nil // No session to update
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	if err := markSessionEnded(event.SessionID); err != nil {
   461→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   462→	}
   463→	return nil
   464→}
   465→
   466→// handleLifecycleSubagentStart handles subagent start: captures pre-task state.
   467→func handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   468→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   469→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-start",
   470→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   471→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   472→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   473→	)
   474→
   475→	// Log context
   476→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Subagent started\n")
   477→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   478→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   479→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Transcript: %s\n", event.SessionRef)
   480→
   481→	// Capture pre-task state
   482→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(event.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   483→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   484→	}
   485→
   486→	return nil
   487→}
   488→
   489→// handleLifecycleSubagentEnd handles subagent end: detects changes, saves task checkpoint.
   490→func handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   491→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   492→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-end",
   493→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   494→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   495→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   496→		slog.String("subagent_id", event.SubagentID),
   497→	)
   498→
   499→	// Extract subagent type and description from tool input
   500→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(event.ToolInput)
   501→
   502→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   503→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(event.SessionRef)
   504→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   505→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   506→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, event.SubagentID)
   507→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   508→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   509→		}
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	// Log context
   513→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Subagent completed\n")
   514→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   515→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   516→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   517→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Agent ID: %s\n", event.SubagentID)
   518→	}
   519→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   520→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Subagent Transcript: %s\n", subagentTranscriptPath)
   521→	}
   522→
   523→	// Extract modified files from subagent transcript
   524→	var modifiedFiles []string
   525→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   526→		transcriptToScan := event.SessionRef
   527→		if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   528→			transcriptToScan = subagentTranscriptPath
   529→		}
   530→		if files, _, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptToScan, 0); fileErr != nil {
   531→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files from subagent: %v\n", fileErr)
   532→		} else {
   533→			modifiedFiles = files
   534→		}
   535→	}
   536→
   537→	// Load pre-task state and detect file changes
   538→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID)
   539→	if err != nil {
   540→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   541→	}
   542→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   543→	if preState != nil {
   544→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   545→	}
   546→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   547→	if err != nil {
   548→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   549→	}
   550→
   551→	// Get repo root and normalize paths
   552→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   553→	if err != nil {
   554→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   555→	}
   556→
   557→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   558→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   559→	if changes != nil {
   560→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   561→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   562→	}
   563→
   564→	// If no changes, skip
   565→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   566→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   567→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   568→		return nil
   569→	}
   570→
   571→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort)
   572→	var checkpointUUID string
   573→	// Use the existing CLI-level checkpoint UUID finder
   574→	mainLines, _, _ := parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(event.SessionRef) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort
   575→	if mainLines != nil {
   576→		checkpointUUID, _ = FindCheckpointUUID(mainLines, event.ToolUseID)
   577→	}
   578→
   579→	// Get git author
   580→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   581→	if err != nil {
   582→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   583→	}
   584→
   585→	// Build task checkpoint context
   586→	strat := GetStrategy()
   587→	agentType := ag.Type()
   588→
   589→	ctx := strategy.TaskStepContext{
   590→		SessionID:              event.SessionID,
   591→		ToolUseID:              event.ToolUseID,
   592→		AgentID:                event.SubagentID,
   593→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   594→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   595→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   596→		TranscriptPath:         event.SessionRef,
   597→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   598→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   599→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   600→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   601→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   602→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   603→		AgentType:              agentType,
   604→	}
   605→
   606→	if err := strat.SaveTaskStep(ctx); err != nil {
   607→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task step: %w", err)
   608→	}
   609→
   610→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   611→	return nil
   612→}
   613→
   614→// --- Helper functions ---
   615→
   616→// resolveTranscriptOffset determines the transcript offset to use for parsing.
   617→// Prefers pre-prompt state, falls back to session state.
   618→func resolveTranscriptOffset(preState *PrePromptState, sessionID string) int {
   619→	if preState != nil && preState.TranscriptOffset > 0 {
   620→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", preState.TranscriptOffset)
   621→		return preState.TranscriptOffset
   622→	}
   623→
   624→	// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   625→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   626→	if loadErr != nil {
   627→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   628→		return 0
   629→	}
   630→	if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   631→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   632→		return sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   633→	}
   634→
   635→	return 0
   636→}
   637→
   638→// updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition updates the session state with the new transcript position
   639→// for the auto-commit strategy.
   640→func updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID string, newPosition int) {
   641→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   642→	if loadErr != nil {
   643→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   644→		return
   645→	}
   646→	if sessionState == nil {
   647→		sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   648→			SessionID: sessionID,
   649→		}
   650→	}
   651→	sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = newPosition
   652→	sessionState.StepCount++
   653→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   654→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   655→	} else {
   656→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   657→			newPosition, sessionState.StepCount)
   658→	}
   659→}
   660→
   661→// createContextFile creates a context.md file for the session checkpoint.
   662→// This is a unified version that works for all agents.
   663→func createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID string, prompts []string, summary string) error {
   664→	var sb strings.Builder
   665→
   666→	sb.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   667→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Session ID: %s\n", sessionID)
   668→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Commit Message: %s\n\n", commitMessage)
   669→
   670→	if len(prompts) > 0 {
   671→		sb.WriteString("## Prompts\n\n")
   672→		for i, p := range prompts {
   673→			fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "### Prompt %d\n\n%s\n\n", i+1, p)
   674→		}
   675→	}
   676→
   677→	if summary != "" {
   678→		sb.WriteString("## Summary\n\n")
   679→		sb.WriteString(summary)
   680→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   681→	}
   682→
   683→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(sb.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   684→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write context file: %w", err)
   685→	}
   686→	return nil
   687→}
   688→
   689→// parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID is a thin wrapper around transcript parsing for checkpoint UUID lookup.
   690→// Returns parsed transcript lines for use with FindCheckpointUUID.
   691→func parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(transcriptPath string) ([]transcriptLine, int, error) {
   692→	lines, total, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   693→	if err != nil {
   694→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("parsing transcript for checkpoint UUID: %w", err)
   695→	}
   696→	return lines, total, nil
   697→}
   698→
   699→// transitionSessionTurnEnd transitions the session phase to IDLE and dispatches turn-end actions.
   700→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   701→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   702→	if loadErr != nil {
   703→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   704→		return
   705→	}
   706→	if turnState == nil {
   707→		return
   708→	}
   709→	if err := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); err != nil {
   710→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end transition failed: %v\n", err)
   711→	}
   712→
   713→	// Always dispatch to strategy for turn-end handling. The strategy reads
   714→	// work items from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not the action list.
   715→	strat := GetStrategy()
   716→	if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   717→		if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState); err != nil {
   718→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   719→		}
   720→	}
   721→
   722→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   723→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   724→	}
   725→}
   726→
   727→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   728→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   729→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   730→	if err != nil {
   731→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   732→	}
   733→	if state == nil {
   734→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   735→	}
   736→
   737→	if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   738→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session stop transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   739→	}
   740→
   741→	now := time.Now()
   742→	state.EndedAt = &now
   743→
   744→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   745→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   746→	}
   747→	return nil
   748→}
   749→
   750→// logFileChanges logs the files modified, created, and deleted during a session.
   751→func logFileChanges(modified, newFiles, deleted []string) {
   752→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(modified))
   753→	for _, file := range modified {
   754→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   755→	}
   756→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
   757→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(newFiles))
   758→		for _, file := range newFiles {
   759→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   760→		}
   761→	}
   762→	if len(deleted) > 0 {
   763→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(deleted))
   764→		for _, file := range deleted {
   765→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   766→		}
   767→	}
   768→}

     1→// hook_registry.go provides hook command registration for agents.
     2→// The lifecycle dispatcher (DispatchLifecycleEvent) handles all lifecycle events.
     3→// PostTodo is the only hook that's handled directly (not via lifecycle dispatcher).
     4→package cli
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"context"
     8→	"errors"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"log/slog"
    11→	"time"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode"
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli"
    16→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    17→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    18→
    19→	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
    20→)
    21→
    22→// agentHookLogCleanup stores the cleanup function for agent hook logging.
    23→// Set by PersistentPreRunE, called by PersistentPostRunE.
    24→var agentHookLogCleanup func()
    25→
    26→// currentHookAgentName stores the agent name for the currently executing hook.
    27→// Set by newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging before calling the handler.
    28→// This allows handlers to know which agent invoked the hook without guessing.
    29→var currentHookAgentName agent.AgentName
    30→
    31→// GetCurrentHookAgent returns the agent for the currently executing hook.
    32→// Returns the agent based on the hook command structure (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
    33→// rather than guessing from directory presence.
    34→// Falls back to GetAgent() if not in a hook context.
    35→func GetCurrentHookAgent() (agent.Agent, error) {
    36→	if currentHookAgentName == "" {
    37→		return nil, errors.New("not in a hook context: agent name not set")
    38→	}
    39→
    40→	ag, err := agent.Get(currentHookAgentName)
    41→	if err != nil {
    42→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("getting hook agent %q: %w", currentHookAgentName, err)
    43→	}
    44→	return ag, nil
    45→}
    46→
    47→// newAgentHooksCmd creates a hooks subcommand for an agent that implements HookHandler.
    48→// It dynamically creates subcommands for each hook the agent supports.
    49→func newAgentHooksCmd(agentName agent.AgentName, handler agent.HookHandler) *cobra.Command {
    50→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    51→		Use:    string(agentName),
    52→		Short:  handler.Description() + " hook handlers",
    53→		Hidden: true,
    54→		PersistentPreRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    55→			agentHookLogCleanup = initHookLogging()
    56→			return nil
    57→		},
    58→		PersistentPostRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    59→			if agentHookLogCleanup != nil {
    60→				agentHookLogCleanup()
    61→			}
    62→			return nil
    63→		},
    64→	}
    65→
    66→	for _, hookName := range handler.GetHookNames() {
    67→		cmd.AddCommand(newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName, hookName))
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	return cmd
    71→}
    72→
    73→// getHookType returns the hook type based on the hook name.
    74→// Returns "subagent" for task-related hooks (pre-task, post-task, post-todo),
    75→// "tool" for tool-related hooks (before-tool, after-tool),
    76→// "agent" for all other agent hooks.
    77→func getHookType(hookName string) string {
    78→	switch hookName {
    79→	case claudecode.HookNamePreTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTodo:
    80→		return "subagent"
    81→	case geminicli.HookNameBeforeTool, geminicli.HookNameAfterTool:
    82→		return "tool"
    83→	default:
    84→		return "agent"
    85→	}
    86→}
    87→
    88→// newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging creates a command for a specific hook verb with structured logging.
    89→// It uses the lifecycle dispatcher (ParseHookEvent → DispatchLifecycleEvent) as the primary path.
    90→// PostTodo is handled directly as it's Claude-specific and not part of the lifecycle dispatcher.
    91→func newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string) *cobra.Command {
    92→	return &cobra.Command{
    93→		Use:    hookName,
    94→		Hidden: true,
    95→		Short:  "Called on " + hookName,
    96→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    97→			// Skip silently if not in a git repository - hooks shouldn't prevent the agent from working
    98→			if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
    99→				return nil
   100→			}
   101→
   102→			// Skip if Entire is not enabled
   103→			enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   104→			if err == nil && !enabled {
   105→				return nil
   106→			}
   107→
   108→			start := time.Now()
   109→
   110→			// Initialize logging context with agent name
   111→			ctx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), agentName)
   112→
   113→			// Get strategy name for logging
   114→			strategyName := GetStrategy().Name()
   115→
   116→			hookType := getHookType(hookName)
   117→
   118→			logging.Debug(ctx, "hook invoked",
   119→				slog.String("hook", hookName),
   120→				slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   121→				slog.String("strategy", strategyName),
   122→			)
   123→
   124→			// Set the current hook agent so handlers can retrieve it
   125→			currentHookAgentName = agentName
   126→			defer func() { currentHookAgentName = "" }()
   127→
   128→			// Use the lifecycle dispatcher for all hooks
   129→			var hookErr error
   130→			ag, agentErr := agent.Get(agentName)
   131→			if agentErr != nil {
   132→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent %q: %w", agentName, agentErr)
   133→			}
   134→
   135→			// Use cmd.InOrStdin() to support testing with cmd.SetIn()
   136→			event, parseErr := ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, cmd.InOrStdin())
   137→			if parseErr != nil {
   138→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook event: %w", parseErr)
   139→			}
   140→
   141→			if event != nil {
   142→				// Lifecycle event — use the generic dispatcher
   143→				hookErr = DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)
   144→			} else if agentName == agent.AgentNameClaudeCode && hookName == claudecode.HookNamePostTodo {
   145→				// PostTodo is Claude-specific: creates incremental checkpoints during subagent execution
   146→				hookErr = handleClaudeCodePostTodo()
   147→			}
   148→			// Other pass-through hooks (nil event, no special handling) are no-ops
   149→
   150→			logging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, "hook completed", start,
   151→				slog.String("hook", hookName),
   152→				slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   153→				slog.String("strategy", strategyName),
   154→				slog.Bool("success", hookErr == nil),
   155→			)
   156→
   157→			return hookErr
   158→		},
   159→	}
   160→}
   161→

     1→// Package claudecode implements the Agent interface for Claude Code.
     2→package claudecode
     3→
     4→import (
     5→	"bufio"
     6→	"encoding/json"

    25→// ClaudeCodeAgent implements the Agent interface for Claude Code.
    26→//
    27→//nolint:revive // ClaudeCodeAgent is clearer than Agent in this context
    28→type ClaudeCodeAgent struct{}
    29→
    30→// NewClaudeCodeAgent creates a new Claude Code agent instance.
    31→func NewClaudeCodeAgent() agent.Agent {
    32→	return &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
    33→}
    34→
    35→// Name returns the agent registry key.
    36→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) Name() agent.AgentName {

    38→}

    40→// Type returns the agent type identifier.

    43→}

    48→}
    49→
    50→// DetectPresence checks if Claude Code is configured in the repository.
    51→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    52→	// Get repo root to check for .claude directory
    53→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    54→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    57→		repoRoot = "."
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	// Check for .claude directory
    61→	claudeDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude")
    62→	if _, err := os.Stat(claudeDir); err == nil {
    63→		return true, nil
    64→	}
    65→	// Check for .claude/settings.json
    66→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", "settings.json")
    67→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    68→		return true, nil
    69→	}
    70→	return false, nil
    71→}
    72→
    73→// GetHookConfigPath returns the path to Claude's hook config file.
    74→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string {
    75→	return ".claude/settings.json"
    76→}
    77→
    78→// SupportsHooks returns true as Claude Code supports lifecycle hooks.
    79→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) SupportsHooks() bool {
    80→	return true
    81→}
    82→
    83→// ParseHookInput parses Claude Code hook input from stdin.
    84→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    85→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	if len(data) == 0 {
    91→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    95→		HookType:  hookType,
    96→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    97→		RawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	// Parse based on hook type
   101→	switch hookType {
   102→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   103→		var raw userPromptSubmitRaw
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   105→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse user prompt submit: %w", err)
   106→		}
   107→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   108→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   109→		input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   110→
   111→	case agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:
   112→		var raw sessionInfoRaw
   113→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   114→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session info: %w", err)
   115→		}
   116→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   117→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   118→
   119→	case agent.HookPreToolUse:
   120→		var raw taskHookInputRaw
   121→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   122→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse pre-tool input: %w", err)
   123→		}
   124→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   125→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   126→		input.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID
   127→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   128→
   129→	case agent.HookPostToolUse:
   130→		var raw postToolHookInputRaw
   131→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   132→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse post-tool input: %w", err)
   133→		}
   134→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   135→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   136→		input.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID
   137→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   138→		// Store agent ID in raw data for Task tool results
   139→		if raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != "" {
   140→			input.RawData["agent_id"] = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID
   141→		}
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	return input, nil
   145→}

   149→	return input.SessionID

   152→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Claude session file.
   153→// Claude names files directly as <id>.jsonl.
   154→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   155→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")
   156→}

   158→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Claude uses for config/state.
