   161→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Claude stores session transcripts.
   162→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   163→	// Check for test environment override
   164→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   165→		return override, nil
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	projectDir := SanitizePathForClaude(repoPath)
   174→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".claude", "projects", projectDir), nil
   175→}
   176→
   177→// ReadSession reads a session from Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   178→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSONL bytes.
   179→// ModifiedFiles is computed by parsing the transcript.
   180→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   181→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   182→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Read the raw JSONL file
   186→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   187→	if err != nil {
   188→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   192→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   198→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   199→		AgentName:     c.Name(),
   200→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   201→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   202→		NativeData:    data,
   203→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(lines),
   204→	}, nil
   205→}
   206→
   207→// WriteSession writes a session to Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   208→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSONL bytes.
   209→// The session must have been created by Claude Code (AgentName check).
   210→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   211→	if session == nil {
   212→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	// Verify this session belongs to Claude Code
   216→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != c.Name() {
   217→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, c.Name())
   218→	}
   219→
   220→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   221→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   225→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	// Write the raw JSONL data
   229→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   230→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	return nil
   234→}
   235→
   236→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Claude Code session.
   237→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   238→	return "claude -r " + sessionID
   239→}
   240→
   241→// Session helper methods - work on AgentSession with Claude's native JSONL data
   242→
   243→// GetLastUserPrompt extracts the last user prompt from the session.
   244→// Requires NativeData to be populated (call ReadSession first).
   245→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetLastUserPrompt(session *agent.AgentSession) string {
   246→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   247→		return ""
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   251→	if err != nil {
   252→		return ""
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	return ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines)
   256→}
   257→
   258→// TruncateAtUUID returns a new session truncated at the given UUID (inclusive).
   259→// This is used for rewind operations to restore transcript state.
   260→// Requires NativeData to be populated.
   261→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) TruncateAtUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, uuid string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   262→	if session == nil {
   263→		return nil, errors.New("session is nil")
   264→	}
   265→
   266→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   267→		return nil, errors.New("session has no native data")
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	if uuid == "" {
   271→		// No truncation needed, return copy
   272→		return &agent.AgentSession{
   273→			SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   274→			AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   275→			RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   276→			SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   277→			StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   278→			NativeData:    session.NativeData,
   279→			ModifiedFiles: session.ModifiedFiles,
   280→		}, nil
   281→	}
   282→
   283→	// Parse, truncate, re-serialize
   284→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	truncated := TruncateAtUUID(lines, uuid)
   290→
   291→	newData, err := SerializeTranscript(truncated)
   292→	if err != nil {
   293→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to serialize truncated transcript: %w", err)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   297→		SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   298→		AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   299→		RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   300→		SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   301→		StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   302→		NativeData:    newData,
   303→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(truncated),
   304→	}, nil
   305→}
   306→
   307→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   308→// for the given tool use ID. Used for task checkpoint rewind.
   309→// Returns the UUID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   310→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FindCheckpointUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   311→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   312→		return "", false
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   316→	if err != nil {
   317→		return "", false
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	return FindCheckpointUUID(lines, toolUseID)
   321→}
   322→
   323→// ReadSessionFromPath is a convenience method that reads a session directly from a file path.
   324→// This is useful when you have the path but not a HookInput.
   325→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSessionFromPath(transcriptPath, sessionID string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   326→	return c.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   327→		SessionID:  sessionID,
   328→		SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   329→	})
   330→}
   331→
   332→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.
   333→// Claude replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash.
   334→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   335→
   336→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {
   337→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   338→}
   339→
   340→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   341→
   342→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of a Claude Code transcript.
   343→// Claude Code uses JSONL format, so position is the number of lines.
   344→// This is a lightweight operation that only counts lines without parsing JSON.
   345→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   346→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   347→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   348→	if path == "" {
   349→		return 0, nil
   350→	}
   351→
   352→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   353→	if err != nil {
   354→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   355→			return 0, nil
   356→		}
   357→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", err)
   358→	}
   359→	defer file.Close()
   360→
   361→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   362→	lineCount := 0
   363→
   364→	for {
   365→		line, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   366→		if err != nil {
   367→			if err == io.EOF {
   368→				if len(line) > 0 {
   369→					lineCount++ // Count final line without trailing newline
   370→				}
   371→				break
   372→			}
   373→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   374→		}
   375→		lineCount++
   376→	}
   377→
   378→	return lineCount, nil
   379→}
   380→
   381→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line number.
   382→// For Claude Code (JSONL format), offset is the starting line number.
   383→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   384→// Returns:
   385→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Claude (from Write/Edit tools)
   386→//   - currentPosition: total number of lines in the file
   387→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   388→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   389→	if path == "" {
   390→		return nil, 0, nil
   391→	}
   392→
   393→	file, openErr := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   394→	if openErr != nil {
   395→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", openErr)
   396→	}
   397→	defer file.Close()
   398→
   399→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   400→	var lines []TranscriptLine
   401→	lineNum := 0
   402→
   403→	for {
   404→		lineData, readErr := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   405→		if readErr != nil && readErr != io.EOF {
   406→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   407→		}
   408→
   409→		if len(lineData) > 0 {
   410→			lineNum++
   411→			if lineNum > startOffset {
   412→				var line TranscriptLine
   413→				if parseErr := json.Unmarshal(lineData, &line); parseErr == nil {
   414→					lines = append(lines, line)
   415→				}
   416→				// Skip malformed lines silently
   417→			}
   418→		}
   419→
   420→		if readErr == io.EOF {
   421→			break
   422→		}
   423→	}
   424→
   425→	return ExtractModifiedFiles(lines), lineNum, nil
   426→}
   427→
   428→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript at line boundaries.
   429→// Claude Code uses JSONL format (one JSON object per line), so chunking
   430→// is done at newline boundaries to preserve message integrity.
   431→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   432→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   433→	if err != nil {
   434→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL transcript: %w", err)
   435→	}
   436→	return chunks, nil
   437→}
   438→
   439→// ReassembleTranscript concatenates JSONL chunks with newlines.
   440→//
   441→
   442→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   443→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   444→}
   445→
   446→// SubagentAwareExtractor interface implementation
   447→
   448→// ExtractAllModifiedFiles extracts files modified by both the main agent and any spawned subagents.
   449→// Claude Code spawns subagents via the Task tool; their transcripts are stored in subagentsDir.
   450→// Returns a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
   451→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {
   452→	return ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)
   453→}
   454→
   455→// CalculateTotalTokenUsage computes token usage including all spawned subagents.
   456→// Claude Code spawns subagents via the Task tool; their transcripts are stored in subagentsDir.
   457→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   458→	return CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)
   459→}
   460→

    27→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Claude Code supports.

    33→// ParseHookEvent translates a Claude Code hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    34→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance.
    35→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    36→	switch hookName {
    37→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    38→		return c.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    39→	case HookNameUserPromptSubmit:
    40→		return c.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    41→	case HookNameStop:
    42→		return c.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    43→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    44→		return c.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    45→	case HookNamePreTask:
    46→		return c.parseSubagentStart(stdin)
    47→	case HookNamePostTask:
    48→		return c.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)
    49→	case HookNamePostTodo:
    50→		// PostTodo is Claude-specific; handled outside the generic dispatcher.
    51→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    52→	default:
    53→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    54→	}
    55→}

    57→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSONL transcript bytes for a session.
    58→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    59→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    62→	}
    63→	return data, nil
    64→}

    66→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
    67→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    68→	lines, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(sessionRef, fromOffset)
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	var prompts []string
    74→	for i := range lines {
    75→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeUser {
    76→			continue
    77→		}
    78→		content := transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[i].Message)
    79→		if content != "" {
    80→			prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content))
    81→		}
    82→	}
    83→	return prompts, nil
    84→}
    85→
    86→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    87→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    88→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	lines, parseErr := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
    94→	if parseErr != nil {
    95→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", parseErr)
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Walk backward to find last assistant text block
    99→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   100→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant {
   101→			continue
   102→		}
   103→		var msg transcript.AssistantMessage
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   105→			continue
   106→		}
   107→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   108→			if block.Type == transcript.ContentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   109→				return block.Text, nil
   110→			}
   111→		}
   112→	}
   113→	return "", nil
   114→}

   221→// --- Transcript flush sentinel ---
   222→
   223→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   224→// entry when the stop hook has been invoked, indicating the transcript is fully flushed.
   225→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   226→
   227→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file for the stop hook sentinel.
   228→// Falls back silently after a timeout.
   229→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   230→	const (
   231→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   232→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   233→		tailBytes    = 4096
   234→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   235→	)
   236→
   237→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "agent.claudecode")
   238→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   239→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   240→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   241→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   242→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   243→			)
   244→			return
   245→		}
   246→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   247→	}
   248→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   249→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   250→	)
   251→}
   252→
   253→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for the sentinel.
   254→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   255→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   256→	if err != nil {
   257→		return false
   258→	}
   259→	defer f.Close()
   260→
   261→	info, err := f.Stat()
   262→	if err != nil {
   263→		return false
   264→	}
   265→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   266→	if offset < 0 {
   267→		offset = 0
   268→	}
   269→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   270→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   271→		return false
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   275→	for _, line := range lines {
   276→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   277→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   278→			continue
   279→		}
   280→
   281→		var entry struct {
   282→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   283→		}
   284→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   285→			continue
   286→		}
   287→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   288→		if err != nil {
   289→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   290→			if err != nil {
   291→				continue
   292→			}
   293→		}
   294→		// Validate timestamp is within acceptable range:
   295→		// - Not too far in the past (before hook started minus skew)
   296→		// - Not too far in the future (after hook started plus skew)
   297→		lowerBound := hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)
   298→		upperBound := hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)
   299→		if ts.After(lowerBound) && ts.Before(upperBound) {
   300→			return true
   301→		}
   302→	}
   303→	return false
   304→}
   305→

     1→package claudecode

     3→import "encoding/json"

     5→// ClaudeSettings represents the .claude/settings.json structure
     6→type ClaudeSettings struct {
     7→	Hooks ClaudeHooks `json:"hooks"`
     8→}
     9→
    10→// ClaudeHooks contains the hook configurations
    11→type ClaudeHooks struct {
    12→	SessionStart     []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"SessionStart,omitempty"`
    13→	SessionEnd       []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"SessionEnd,omitempty"`
    14→	UserPromptSubmit []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"UserPromptSubmit,omitempty"`
    15→	Stop             []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"Stop,omitempty"`
    16→	PreToolUse       []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"PreToolUse,omitempty"`
    17→	PostToolUse      []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"PostToolUse,omitempty"`
    18→}
    19→
    20→// ClaudeHookMatcher matches hooks to specific patterns
    21→type ClaudeHookMatcher struct {
    22→	Matcher string            `json:"matcher"`
    23→	Hooks   []ClaudeHookEntry `json:"hooks"`
    24→}
    25→
    26→// ClaudeHookEntry represents a single hook command
    27→type ClaudeHookEntry struct {
    28→	Type    string `json:"type"`
    29→	Command string `json:"command"`
    30→}
    31→
    32→// sessionInfoRaw is the JSON structure from SessionStart/SessionEnd/Stop hooks
    33→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
    34→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    35→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    36→}
    37→
    38→// userPromptSubmitRaw is the JSON structure from UserPromptSubmit hooks.
    39→// Unlike other session hooks, this includes the user's prompt text.
    40→type userPromptSubmitRaw struct {
    41→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`

    44→}
    45→

    21→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
    22→func init() {
    23→	agent.Register(agent.AgentNameGemini, NewGeminiCLIAgent)

    25→
    26→// GeminiCLIAgent implements the Agent interface for Gemini CLI.
    27→//
    28→//nolint:revive // GeminiCLIAgent is clearer than Agent in this context
    29→type GeminiCLIAgent struct{}

    31→func NewGeminiCLIAgent() agent.Agent {
    32→	return &GeminiCLIAgent{}
    33→}
    34→
    35→// Name returns the agent registry key.
    36→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) Name() agent.AgentName {
    37→	return agent.AgentNameGemini
    38→}

    43→}

    45→// Description returns a human-readable description.

    50→// DetectPresence checks if Gemini CLI is configured in the repository.
    51→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    52→	// Get repo root to check for .gemini directory
    53→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    54→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    57→		repoRoot = "."
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	// Check for .gemini directory
    61→	geminiDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini")
    62→	if _, err := os.Stat(geminiDir); err == nil {
    63→		return true, nil
    64→	}
    65→	// Check for .gemini/settings.json
    66→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", "settings.json")
    67→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    68→		return true, nil
    69→	}
    70→	return false, nil
    71→}
    72→
    73→// GetHookConfigPath returns the path to Gemini's hook config file.
    74→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string {
    75→	return ".gemini/settings.json"
    76→}
    77→
    78→// SupportsHooks returns true as Gemini CLI supports lifecycle hooks.
    79→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) SupportsHooks() bool {
    80→	return true
    81→}
    82→
    83→// ParseHookInput parses Gemini CLI hook input from stdin.
    84→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    85→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	if len(data) == 0 {
    91→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    95→		HookType:  hookType,
    96→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    97→		RawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	// Parse based on hook type
   101→	switch hookType {
   102→	case agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:
   103→		var raw sessionInfoRaw
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   105→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session info: %w", err)
   106→		}
   107→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   108→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   109→		// Store Gemini-specific fields in RawData
   110→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   111→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   112→		if raw.Source != "" {
   113→			input.RawData["source"] = raw.Source
   114→		}
   115→		if raw.Reason != "" {
   116→			input.RawData["reason"] = raw.Reason
   117→		}
   118→
   119→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   120→		// BeforeAgent is Gemini's equivalent to Claude's UserPromptSubmit
   121→		// It provides the user's prompt in the "prompt" field
   122→		var raw agentHookInputRaw
   123→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   124→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse agent hook input: %w", err)
   125→		}
   126→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   127→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   128→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   129→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   130→		if raw.Prompt != "" {
   131→			input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   132→			input.RawData["prompt"] = raw.Prompt
   133→		}
   134→
   135→	case agent.HookPreToolUse, agent.HookPostToolUse:
   136→		var raw toolHookInputRaw
   137→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   138→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse tool hook input: %w", err)
   139→		}
   140→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   141→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   142→		input.ToolName = raw.ToolName
   143→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   144→		if hookType == agent.HookPostToolUse {
   145→			input.ToolResponse = raw.ToolResponse
   146→		}
   147→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   148→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   149→	}
   150→
   151→	return input, nil
   152→}
   153→
   154→// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.
   155→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
   156→	return input.SessionID
   157→}
   158→
   159→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Gemini uses for config/state.
   160→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".gemini"} }
   161→
   162→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Gemini session file.
   163→// Gemini names files as session-<date>-<shortid>.json where shortid is the first 8 chars
   164→// of the session UUID. This searches for an existing file matching the pattern, falling
   165→// back to <dir>/<id>.json if no match is found.
   166→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   167→	// Try to find existing file matching Gemini's naming convention:
   168→	// session-*-<first8chars>.json
   169→	shortID := agentSessionID
   170→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
   171→		shortID = shortID[:8]
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	pattern := filepath.Join(sessionDir, "session-*-"+shortID+".json")
   175→	matches, err := filepath.Glob(pattern)
   176→	if err == nil && len(matches) > 0 {
   177→		// Return the most recent match (last alphabetically, since date is in the name)
   178→		return matches[len(matches)-1]
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Fallback: construct a default path
   182→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".json")
   183→}
   184→
   185→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Gemini stores session transcripts.
   186→// Gemini stores sessions in ~/.gemini/tmp/<project-hash>/chats/
   187→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   188→	// Check for test environment override
   189→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   190→		return override, nil
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   194→	if err != nil {
   195→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Gemini uses a hash of the project path for the directory name
   199→	projectDir := SanitizePathForGemini(repoPath)
   200→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".gemini", "tmp", projectDir, "chats"), nil
   201→}
   202→
   203→// ReadSession reads a session from Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   204→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSON bytes.
   205→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   206→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   207→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	// Read the raw JSON file
   211→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   212→	if err != nil {
   213→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   217→	modifiedFiles, err := ExtractModifiedFiles(data)
   218→	if err != nil {
   219→		// Non-fatal: we can still return the session without modified files
   220→		modifiedFiles = nil
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   224→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   225→		AgentName:     g.Name(),
   226→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   227→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   228→		NativeData:    data,
   229→		ModifiedFiles: modifiedFiles,
   230→	}, nil
   231→}
   232→
   233→// WriteSession writes a session to Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   234→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSON bytes.
   235→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   236→	if session == nil {
   237→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	// Verify this session belongs to Gemini CLI
   241→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != g.Name() {
   242→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, g.Name())
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   246→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   250→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Write the raw JSON data
   254→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   255→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	return nil
   259→}
   260→
   261→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Gemini CLI session.
   262→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   263→	return "gemini --resume " + sessionID
   264→}
   265→
   266→// SanitizePathForGemini converts a path to Gemini's project directory format.
   267→// Gemini uses a hash-like sanitization similar to Claude.
   268→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   269→
   270→func SanitizePathForGemini(path string) string {
   271→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   272→}
   273→
   274→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   275→
   276→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current message count of a Gemini transcript.
   277→// Gemini uses JSON format with a messages array, so position is the message count.
   278→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   279→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   280→	if path == "" {
   281→		return 0, nil
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   287→			return 0, nil
   288→		}
   289→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	if len(data) == 0 {
   293→		return 0, nil
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   297→	if err != nil {
   298→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	return len(transcript.Messages), nil
   302→}
   303→
   304→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given message index.
   305→// For Gemini (JSON format), offset is the starting message index.
   306→// Returns:
   307→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Gemini (from Write/Edit tools)
   308→//   - currentPosition: total number of messages in the transcript
   309→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   310→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   311→	if path == "" {
   312→		return nil, 0, nil
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   316→	if readErr != nil {
   317→		if os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
   318→			return nil, 0, nil
   319→		}
   320→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   321→	}
   322→
   323→	if len(data) == 0 {
   324→		return nil, 0, nil
   325→	}
   326→
   327→	transcript, parseErr := ParseTranscript(data)
   328→	if parseErr != nil {
   329→		return nil, 0, parseErr
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	totalMessages := len(transcript.Messages)
   333→
   334→	// Extract files from messages starting at startOffset
   335→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   336→	for i := startOffset; i < len(transcript.Messages); i++ {
   337→		msg := transcript.Messages[i]
   338→		// Only process gemini messages (assistant messages)
   339→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeGemini {
   340→			continue
   341→		}
   342→
   343→		// Process tool calls in this message
   344→		for _, toolCall := range msg.ToolCalls {
   345→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
   346→			isModifyTool := false
   347→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
   348→				if toolCall.Name == name {
   349→					isModifyTool = true
   350→					break
   351→				}
   352→			}
   353→
   354→			if !isModifyTool {
   355→				continue
   356→			}
   357→
   358→			// Extract file path from args map
   359→			var file string
   360→			if fp, ok := toolCall.Args["file_path"].(string); ok && fp != "" {
   361→				file = fp
   362→			} else if p, ok := toolCall.Args["path"].(string); ok && p != "" {
   363→				file = p
   364→			} else if fn, ok := toolCall.Args["filename"].(string); ok && fn != "" {
   365→				file = fn
   366→			}
   367→
   368→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   369→				fileSet[file] = true
   370→				files = append(files, file)
   371→			}
   372→		}
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	return files, totalMessages, nil
   376→}
   377→
   378→// ChunkTranscript splits a Gemini JSON transcript by distributing messages across chunks.
   379→// Gemini uses JSON format with a {"messages": [...]} structure, so chunking splits
   380→// the messages array while preserving the JSON structure in each chunk.
   381→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   382→	var transcript GeminiTranscript
   383→	if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &transcript); err != nil {
   384→		// Fall back to JSONL chunking if not valid Gemini JSON
   385→		chunks, chunkErr := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   386→		if chunkErr != nil {
   387→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk as JSONL: %w", chunkErr)
   388→		}
   389→		return chunks, nil
   390→	}
   391→
   392→	if len(transcript.Messages) == 0 {
   393→		return [][]byte{content}, nil
   394→	}
   395→
   396→	var chunks [][]byte
   397→	var currentMessages []GeminiMessage
   398→	currentSize := len(`{"messages":[]}`) // Base JSON structure size
   399→
   400→	for i, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   401→		// Marshal message to get its size
   402→		msgBytes, err := json.Marshal(msg)
   403→		if err != nil {
   404→			logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to marshal Gemini message during chunking",
   405→				slog.Int("message_index", i),
   406→				slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   407→			)
   408→			continue
   409→		}
   410→		msgSize := len(msgBytes) + 1 // +1 for comma separator
   411→
   412→		if currentSize+msgSize > maxSize && len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   413→			// Save current chunk
   414→			chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   415→			if err != nil {
   416→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal chunk: %w", err)
   417→			}
   418→			chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   419→
   420→			// Start new chunk
   421→			currentMessages = nil
   422→			currentSize = len(`{"messages":[]}`)
   423→		}
   424→
   425→		currentMessages = append(currentMessages, msg)
   426→		currentSize += msgSize
   427→	}
   428→
   429→	// Add the last chunk
   430→	if len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   431→		chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   432→		if err != nil {
   433→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal final chunk: %w", err)
   434→		}
   435→		chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   436→	}
   437→
   438→	// Ensure we created at least one chunk (could be empty if all messages failed to marshal)
   439→	if len(chunks) == 0 {
   440→		return nil, errors.New("failed to create any chunks: all messages failed to marshal")
   441→	}
   442→
   443→	return chunks, nil
   444→}
   445→
   446→// ReassembleTranscript merges Gemini JSON chunks by combining their message arrays.
   447→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   448→	var allMessages []GeminiMessage
   449→
   450→	for _, chunk := range chunks {
   451→		var transcript GeminiTranscript
   452→		if err := json.Unmarshal(chunk, &transcript); err != nil {
   453→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal chunk: %w", err)
   454→		}
   455→		allMessages = append(allMessages, transcript.Messages...)
   456→	}
   457→
   458→	result, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: allMessages})
   459→	if err != nil {
   460→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal reassembled transcript: %w", err)
   461→	}
   462→	return result, nil
   463→}

     1→package geminicli
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"io"

     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// Compile-time interface assertions for new interfaces.
    13→var (
    14→	_ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*GeminiCLIAgent)(nil)
    15→	_ agent.TokenCalculator    = (*GeminiCLIAgent)(nil)

    17→
    18→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Gemini CLI supports.
    19→// Delegates to GetHookNames for backward compatibility.
    20→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) HookNames() []string {
    21→	return g.GetHookNames()
    22→}
    23→
    24→// ParseHookEvent translates a Gemini CLI hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    25→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    26→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    27→	switch hookName {
    28→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    29→		return g.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    30→	case HookNameBeforeAgent:
    31→		return g.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    32→	case HookNameAfterAgent:
    33→		return g.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    34→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    35→		return g.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    36→	case HookNamePreCompress:
    37→		return g.parseCompaction(stdin)
    38→	case HookNameBeforeTool, HookNameAfterTool, HookNameBeforeModel,
    39→		HookNameAfterModel, HookNameBeforeToolSelection, HookNameNotification:
    40→		// Acknowledged hooks with no lifecycle action
    41→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    42→	default:
    43→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    44→	}
    45→}
    46→
    47→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSON transcript bytes for a session.
    48→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    49→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    50→	if err != nil {
    51→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    52→	}
    53→	return data, nil
    54→}
    55→
    56→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given message offset.
    57→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    58→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    59→	if err != nil {
    60→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	t, parseErr := ParseTranscript(data)
    64→	if parseErr != nil {
    65→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", parseErr)
    66→	}
    67→
    68→	var prompts []string
    69→	for i := fromOffset; i < len(t.Messages); i++ {
    70→		msg := t.Messages[i]
    71→		if msg.Type == MessageTypeUser && msg.Content != "" {
    72→			prompts = append(prompts, msg.Content)
    73→		}
    74→	}
    75→	return prompts, nil
    76→}
    77→
    78→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    79→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    80→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    81→	if err != nil {
    82→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    83→	}
    84→	return ExtractLastAssistantMessage(data)
    85→}
    86→
    87→// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given message offset.
    88→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
    89→	return CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(sessionRef, fromOffset)
    90→}
    91→
    92→// --- Internal hook parsing functions ---
    93→
    94→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    95→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    96→	if err != nil {
    97→		return nil, err

   104→	}, nil
   105→}

   112→	return &agent.Event{

   118→	}, nil

   120→

     1→package geminicli
     2→
     3→import "encoding/json"
     4→
     5→// GeminiSettings represents the .gemini/settings.json structure
     6→type GeminiSettings struct {
     7→	HooksConfig GeminiHooksConfig `json:"hooksConfig,omitempty"`
     8→	Hooks       GeminiHooks       `json:"hooks,omitempty"`
     9→}
    10→
    11→// GeminiHooksConfig contains tool-related settings
    12→type GeminiHooksConfig struct {
    13→	Enabled bool `json:"enabled,omitempty"`
    14→}
    15→
    16→// GeminiHooks contains all hook configurations
    17→type GeminiHooks struct {
    18→	// Hooks are only executed when hooksConfig.enabled is true in .gemini/settings.json.
    19→	SessionStart        []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"SessionStart,omitempty"`
    20→	SessionEnd          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"SessionEnd,omitempty"`
    21→	BeforeAgent         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeAgent,omitempty"`
    22→	AfterAgent          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterAgent,omitempty"`
    23→	BeforeModel         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeModel,omitempty"`
    24→	AfterModel          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterModel,omitempty"`
    25→	BeforeToolSelection []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeToolSelection,omitempty"`
    26→	BeforeTool          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeTool,omitempty"`
    27→	AfterTool           []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterTool,omitempty"`
    28→	PreCompress         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"PreCompress,omitempty"`
    29→	Notification        []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"Notification,omitempty"`
    30→}
    31→
    32→// GeminiHookMatcher matches hooks to specific patterns
    33→type GeminiHookMatcher struct {
    34→	Matcher string            `json:"matcher,omitempty"`
    35→	Hooks   []GeminiHookEntry `json:"hooks"`
    36→}
    37→
    38→// GeminiHookEntry represents a single hook command.
    39→// Unlike Claude Code, Gemini CLI requires a "name" field for each hook entry.
    40→type GeminiHookEntry struct {
    41→	Name    string `json:"name"`
    42→	Type    string `json:"type"`
    43→	Command string `json:"command"`
    44→}
    45→
    46→// sessionInfoRaw is the JSON structure from SessionStart/SessionEnd hooks
    47→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
    48→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    49→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    50→	Cwd            string `json:"cwd"`
    51→	HookEventName  string `json:"hook_event_name"`
    52→	Timestamp      string `json:"timestamp"`
    53→	Source         string `json:"source,omitempty"` // For SessionStart: startup, resume, clear
    54→	Reason         string `json:"reason,omitempty"` // For SessionEnd: exit, logout
    55→}
    56→
    57→// agentHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from BeforeAgent/AfterAgent hooks.
    58→// BeforeAgent includes the user's prompt, similar to Claude's UserPromptSubmit.
    59→type agentHookInputRaw struct {
    60→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    61→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    62→	Cwd            string `json:"cwd"`
    63→	HookEventName  string `json:"hook_event_name"`
    64→	Timestamp      string `json:"timestamp"`
    65→	Prompt         string `json:"prompt,omitempty"` // User's prompt (BeforeAgent only)
    66→}
    67→
    68→// toolHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from BeforeTool/AfterTool hooks
    69→type toolHookInputRaw struct {
    70→	SessionID      string          `json:"session_id"`
    71→	TranscriptPath string          `json:"transcript_path"`
    72→	Cwd            string          `json:"cwd"`
    73→	HookEventName  string          `json:"hook_event_name"`
    74→	Timestamp      string          `json:"timestamp"`
    75→	ToolName       string          `json:"tool_name"`

    77→	ToolResponse   json.RawMessage `json:"tool_response,omitempty"` // Only for AfterTool
    78→}
    79→
    80→// Tool names used in Gemini CLI that modify files
    81→// Note: Gemini CLI uses different names in different contexts:
    82→// - Internal/transcript names: write_file, replace
    83→// - Display names: WriteFile, Edit
    84→const (

    86→	ToolEditFile  = "edit_file"
    87→	ToolSaveFile  = "save_file"
    88→	ToolReplace   = "replace"

    91→// FileModificationTools lists tools that create or modify files in Gemini CLI

    97→}
    98→
    99→// geminiMessageTokens represents token usage from a Gemini API response.
   100→// This is specific to Gemini's API format where each message has a tokens object.
   101→type geminiMessageTokens struct {
   102→	Input    int `json:"input"`
   103→	Output   int `json:"output"`
   104→	Cached   int `json:"cached"`
   105→	Thoughts int `json:"thoughts"`
   106→	Tool     int `json:"tool"`
   107→	Total    int `json:"total"`
   108→}
   109→
   110→// geminiMessageWithTokens represents a Gemini message with token usage data.
   111→// Used for extracting token counts from Gemini transcripts.
   112→type geminiMessageWithTokens struct {
   113→	ID     string               `json:"id"`
   114→	Type   string               `json:"type"`
   115→	Tokens *geminiMessageTokens `json:"tokens,omitempty"`
   116→}

    60→// InstallHooks installs Claude Code hooks in .claude/settings.json.
    61→// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
    62→// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    63→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    64→	// Use repo root instead of CWD to find .claude directory
    65→	// This ensures hooks are installed correctly when run from a subdirectory
    66→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    67→	if err != nil {
    68→		// Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo (e.g., during tests)
    69→		repoRoot, err = os.Getwd() //nolint:forbidigo // Intentional fallback when RepoRoot() fails (tests run outside git repos)
    70→		if err != nil {
    71→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get current directory: %w", err)
    72→		}
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
    76→
    77→	// Read existing settings if they exist
    78→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
    79→
    80→	// rawHooks preserves unknown hook types (e.g., "Notification", "SubagentStop")
    81→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
    82→
    83→	// rawPermissions preserves unknown permission fields (e.g., "ask")
    84→	var rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage

    98→				return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse permissions in settings.json: %w", err)
    99→			}
   100→		}
   101→	} else {

   103→	}
   104→

   115→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", &sessionEnd)

   118→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", &preToolUse)
   119→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", &postToolUse)

   122→	if force {
   123→		sessionStart = removeEntireHooks(sessionStart)
   124→		sessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(sessionEnd)
   125→		stop = removeEntireHooks(stop)
   126→		userPromptSubmit = removeEntireHooks(userPromptSubmit)
   127→		preToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(preToolUse)
   128→		postToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(postToolUse)
   129→	}

   131→	// Define hook commands

   133→	if localDev {
   134→		sessionStartCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code session-start"
   135→		sessionEndCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code session-end"
   136→		stopCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code stop"
   137→		userPromptSubmitCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code user-prompt-submit"
   138→		preTaskCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code pre-task"
   139→		postTaskCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code post-task"
   140→		postTodoCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code post-todo"
   141→	} else {
   142→		sessionStartCmd = "entire hooks claude-code session-start"
   143→		sessionEndCmd = "entire hooks claude-code session-end"
   144→		stopCmd = "entire hooks claude-code stop"
   145→		userPromptSubmitCmd = "entire hooks claude-code user-prompt-submit"
   146→		preTaskCmd = "entire hooks claude-code pre-task"
   147→		postTaskCmd = "entire hooks claude-code post-task"
   148→		postTodoCmd = "entire hooks claude-code post-todo"
   149→	}
   150→
   151→	count := 0

   153→	// Add hooks if they don't exist

   156→		count++
   157→	}
   158→	if !hookCommandExists(sessionEnd, sessionEndCmd) {

   160→		count++
   161→	}

   173→	}

   176→		count++

   179→		postToolUse = addHookToMatcher(postToolUse, "TodoWrite", postTodoCmd)
   180→		count++
   181→	}

   183→	// Add permissions.deny rule if not present
   184→	permissionsChanged := false
   185→	var denyRules []string
   186→	if denyRaw, ok := rawPermissions["deny"]; ok {
   187→		if err := json.Unmarshal(denyRaw, &denyRules); err != nil {
   188→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse permissions.deny in settings.json: %w", err)

   190→	}

   195→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal permissions.deny: %w", err)
   196→		}
   197→		rawPermissions["deny"] = denyJSON
   198→		permissionsChanged = true
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	if count == 0 && !permissionsChanged {

   203→	}

   205→	// Marshal modified hook types back to rawHooks
   206→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", sessionStart)
   207→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", sessionEnd)
   208→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "Stop", stop)
   209→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "UserPromptSubmit", userPromptSubmit)
   210→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", preToolUse)
   211→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", postToolUse)
   212→
   213→	// Marshal hooks and update raw settings
   214→	hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)
   215→	if err != nil {
   216→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   217→	}
   218→	rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   219→
   220→	// Marshal permissions and update raw settings
   221→	permJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawPermissions)
   222→	if err != nil {
   223→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal permissions: %w", err)
   224→	}
   225→	rawSettings["permissions"] = permJSON
   226→
   227→	// Write back to file
   228→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(settingsPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   229→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .claude directory: %w", err)
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   233→	if err != nil {
   234→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   238→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	return count, nil
   242→}
   243→
   244→// parseHookType parses a specific hook type from rawHooks into the target slice.
   245→// Silently ignores parse errors (leaves target unchanged).
   246→func parseHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, target *[]ClaudeHookMatcher) {
   247→	if data, ok := rawHooks[hookType]; ok {
   248→		//nolint:errcheck,gosec // Intentionally ignoring parse errors - leave target as nil/empty
   249→		json.Unmarshal(data, target)
   250→	}
   251→}
   252→
   253→// marshalHookType marshals a hook type back to rawHooks.
   254→// If the slice is empty, removes the key from rawHooks.
   255→func marshalHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) {
   256→	if len(matchers) == 0 {
   257→		delete(rawHooks, hookType)
   258→		return
   259→	}
   260→	data, err := json.Marshal(matchers)
   261→	if err != nil {
   262→		return // Silently ignore marshal errors (shouldn't happen)
   263→	}
   264→	rawHooks[hookType] = data
   265→}
   266→
   267→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Claude Code settings.
   268→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   269→	// Use repo root to find .claude directory when run from a subdirectory
   270→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   271→	if err != nil {
   272→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   273→	}
   274→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
   275→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   276→	if err != nil {
   277→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No settings file means nothing to uninstall
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   281→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   282→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse settings.json: %w", err)
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// rawHooks preserves unknown hook types (e.g., "Notification", "SubagentStop")
   286→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
   287→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
   288→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
   289→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks: %w", err)
   290→		}
   291→	}
   292→	if rawHooks == nil {
   293→		rawHooks = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Parse only the hook types we need to modify
   297→	var sessionStart, sessionEnd, stop, userPromptSubmit, preToolUse, postToolUse []ClaudeHookMatcher
   298→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", &sessionStart)
   299→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", &sessionEnd)
   300→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "Stop", &stop)
   301→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "UserPromptSubmit", &userPromptSubmit)
   302→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", &preToolUse)
   303→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", &postToolUse)
   304→
   305→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   306→	sessionStart = removeEntireHooks(sessionStart)
   307→	sessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(sessionEnd)
   308→	stop = removeEntireHooks(stop)
   309→	userPromptSubmit = removeEntireHooks(userPromptSubmit)
   310→	preToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(preToolUse)
   311→	postToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(postToolUse)
   312→
   313→	// Marshal modified hook types back to rawHooks
   314→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", sessionStart)
   315→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", sessionEnd)
   316→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "Stop", stop)
   317→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "UserPromptSubmit", userPromptSubmit)
   318→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", preToolUse)
   319→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", postToolUse)
   320→
   321→	// Also remove the metadata deny rule from permissions
   322→	var rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage
   323→	if permRaw, ok := rawSettings["permissions"]; ok {
   324→		if err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &rawPermissions); err != nil {
   325→			// If parsing fails, just skip permissions cleanup
   326→			rawPermissions = nil
   327→		}
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	if rawPermissions != nil {
   331→		if denyRaw, ok := rawPermissions["deny"]; ok {
   332→			var denyRules []string
   333→			if err := json.Unmarshal(denyRaw, &denyRules); err == nil {
   334→				// Filter out the metadata deny rule
   335→				filteredRules := make([]string, 0, len(denyRules))
   336→				for _, rule := range denyRules {
   337→					if rule != metadataDenyRule {
   338→						filteredRules = append(filteredRules, rule)
   339→					}
   340→				}
   341→				if len(filteredRules) > 0 {
   342→					denyJSON, err := json.Marshal(filteredRules)
   343→					if err == nil {
   344→						rawPermissions["deny"] = denyJSON
   345→					}
   346→				} else {
   347→					// Remove empty deny array
   348→					delete(rawPermissions, "deny")
   349→				}
   350→			}
   351→		}
   352→
   353→		// If permissions is empty, remove it entirely
   354→		if len(rawPermissions) > 0 {
   355→			permJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawPermissions)
   356→			if err == nil {
   357→				rawSettings["permissions"] = permJSON
   358→			}
   359→		} else {
   360→			delete(rawSettings, "permissions")
   361→		}
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	// Marshal hooks back (preserving unknown hook types)
   365→	if len(rawHooks) > 0 {
   366→		hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)
   367→		if err != nil {
   368→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   369→		}
   370→		rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   371→	} else {
   372→		delete(rawSettings, "hooks")
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	// Write back
   376→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   377→	if err != nil {
   378→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   379→	}
   380→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   381→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   382→	}
   383→	return nil
   384→}
   385→
   386→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.
   387→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   388→	// Use repo root to find .claude directory when run from a subdirectory
   389→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   390→	if err != nil {
   391→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   392→	}
   393→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
   394→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   395→	if err != nil {
   396→		return false
   397→	}
   398→
   399→	var settings ClaudeSettings
   400→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   401→		return false
   402→	}
   403→
   404→	// Check for at least one of our hooks (new or old format)
   405→	return hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks claude-code stop") ||
   406→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code stop") ||
   407→		// Backwards compatibility: check for old hook formats
   408→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks claudecode stop") ||
   409→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claudecode stop") ||
   410→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire rewind claude-hook --stop") ||
   411→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go rewind claude-hook --stop")
   412→}
   413→
   414→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Claude Code supports.
   415→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   416→	return []agent.HookType{
   417→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   418→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   419→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   420→		agent.HookStop,
   421→		agent.HookPreToolUse,
   422→		agent.HookPostToolUse,
   423→	}
   424→}
   425→
   426→// Helper functions for hook management
   427→
   428→func hookCommandExists(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, command string) bool {
   429→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   430→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   431→			if hook.Command == command {
   432→				return true
   433→			}
   434→		}
   435→	}
   436→	return false
   437→}
   438→
   439→func hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) bool {
   440→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   441→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   442→			for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   443→				if hook.Command == command {
   444→					return true
   445→				}
   446→			}
   447→		}
   448→	}
   449→	return false
   450→}
   451→
   452→func addHookToMatcher(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   453→	entry := ClaudeHookEntry{
   454→		Type:    "command",
   455→		Command: command,
   456→	}
   457→
   458→	// If no matcher name, add to a matcher with empty string
   459→	if matcherName == "" {
   460→		for i, matcher := range matchers {
   461→			if matcher.Matcher == "" {
   462→				matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   463→				return matchers
   464→			}
   465→		}
   466→		return append(matchers, ClaudeHookMatcher{
   467→			Matcher: "",
   468→			Hooks:   []ClaudeHookEntry{entry},
   469→		})
   470→	}
   471→
   472→	// Find or create matcher with the given name
   473→	for i, matcher := range matchers {
   474→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   475→			matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   476→			return matchers
   477→		}
   478→	}
   479→
   480→	return append(matchers, ClaudeHookMatcher{
   481→		Matcher: matcherName,
   482→		Hooks:   []ClaudeHookEntry{entry},
   483→	})
   484→}
   485→
   486→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook (old or new format)
   487→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   488→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   489→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   490→			return true
   491→		}
   492→	}
   493→	return false
   494→}
   495→
   496→// removeEntireHooks removes all Entire hooks from a list of matchers (for simple hooks like Stop)
   497→func removeEntireHooks(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   498→	result := make([]ClaudeHookMatcher, 0, len(matchers))
   499→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   500→		filteredHooks := make([]ClaudeHookEntry, 0, len(matcher.Hooks))
   501→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   502→			if !isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   503→				filteredHooks = append(filteredHooks, hook)
   504→			}
   505→		}
   506→		// Only keep the matcher if it has hooks remaining
   507→		if len(filteredHooks) > 0 {
   508→			matcher.Hooks = filteredHooks
   509→			result = append(result, matcher)
   510→		}
   511→	}
   512→	return result
   513→}
   514→
   515→// removeEntireHooksFromMatchers removes Entire hooks from tool-use matchers (PreToolUse, PostToolUse)
   516→// This handles the nested structure where hooks are grouped by tool matcher (e.g., "Task", "TodoWrite")
   517→func removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   518→	// Same logic as removeEntireHooks - both work on the same structure
   519→	return removeEntireHooks(matchers)
   520→}

    64→// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    65→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    66→	// Use repo root instead of CWD to find .gemini directory
    67→	// This ensures hooks are installed correctly when run from a subdirectory
    68→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()

    78→

   100→			}

   108→	}
   109→

   112→	hooksConfig.Enabled = true

   244→// parseGeminiHookType parses a specific hook type from rawHooks into the target slice.
   245→// Silently ignores parse errors (leaves target unchanged).
   246→func parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, target *[]GeminiHookMatcher) {
   247→	if data, ok := rawHooks[hookType]; ok {
   248→		//nolint:errcheck,gosec // Intentionally ignoring parse errors - leave target as nil/empty
   249→		json.Unmarshal(data, target)
   250→	}
   251→}
   252→
   253→// marshalGeminiHookType marshals a hook type back to rawHooks.
   254→// If the slice is empty, removes the key from rawHooks.
   255→func marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) {
   256→	if len(matchers) == 0 {
   257→		delete(rawHooks, hookType)
   258→		return
   259→	}
   260→	data, err := json.Marshal(matchers)
   261→	if err != nil {
   262→		return // Silently ignore marshal errors (shouldn't happen)
   263→	}
   264→	rawHooks[hookType] = data
   265→}

   267→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Gemini CLI settings.
   268→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   269→	// Use repo root to find .gemini directory when run from a subdirectory
   270→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   271→	if err != nil {
   272→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   273→	}
   274→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", GeminiSettingsFileName)
   275→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   276→	if err != nil {
   277→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No settings file means nothing to uninstall
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   281→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   282→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse settings.json: %w", err)
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// rawHooks preserves unknown hook types
   286→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
   287→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
   288→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
   289→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks: %w", err)
   290→		}
   291→	}
   292→	if rawHooks == nil {
   293→		rawHooks = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Parse only the hook types we need to modify
   297→	var sessionStart, sessionEnd, beforeAgent, afterAgent []GeminiHookMatcher
   298→	var beforeModel, afterModel, beforeToolSelection []GeminiHookMatcher
   299→	var beforeTool, afterTool, preCompress, notification []GeminiHookMatcher
   300→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", &sessionStart)
   301→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", &sessionEnd)
   302→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeAgent", &beforeAgent)
   303→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterAgent", &afterAgent)
   304→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeModel", &beforeModel)
   305→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterModel", &afterModel)
   306→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeToolSelection", &beforeToolSelection)
   307→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeTool", &beforeTool)
   308→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterTool", &afterTool)
   309→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompress", &preCompress)
   310→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "Notification", &notification)
   311→
   312→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   313→	sessionStart = removeEntireHooks(sessionStart)
   314→	sessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(sessionEnd)
   315→	beforeAgent = removeEntireHooks(beforeAgent)
   316→	afterAgent = removeEntireHooks(afterAgent)
   317→	beforeModel = removeEntireHooks(beforeModel)
   318→	afterModel = removeEntireHooks(afterModel)
   319→	beforeToolSelection = removeEntireHooks(beforeToolSelection)
   320→	beforeTool = removeEntireHooks(beforeTool)
   321→	afterTool = removeEntireHooks(afterTool)
   322→	preCompress = removeEntireHooks(preCompress)
   323→	notification = removeEntireHooks(notification)
   324→
   325→	// Marshal modified hook types back to rawHooks
   326→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", sessionStart)
   327→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", sessionEnd)
   328→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeAgent", beforeAgent)
   329→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterAgent", afterAgent)
   330→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeModel", beforeModel)
   331→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterModel", afterModel)
   332→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeToolSelection", beforeToolSelection)
   333→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeTool", beforeTool)
   334→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterTool", afterTool)
   335→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompress", preCompress)
   336→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "Notification", notification)
   337→
   338→	// Marshal hooks back (preserving unknown hook types)
   339→	if len(rawHooks) > 0 {
   340→		hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)
   341→		if err != nil {
   342→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   343→		}
   344→		rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   345→	} else {
   346→		delete(rawSettings, "hooks")
   347→	}
   348→
   349→	// Write back
   350→	output, err := json.MarshalIndent(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   351→	if err != nil {
   352→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   353→	}
   354→
   355→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   356→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   357→	}
   358→	return nil
   359→}
   360→
   361→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.
   362→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   363→	// Use repo root to find .gemini directory when run from a subdirectory
   364→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   365→	if err != nil {
   366→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   367→	}
   368→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", GeminiSettingsFileName)
   369→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   370→	if err != nil {
   371→		return false
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	var settings GeminiSettings
   375→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   376→		return false
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	// Check for at least one of our hooks using isEntireHook (works for both localDev and production)
   380→	return hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.SessionStart) ||
   381→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd) ||
   382→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeAgent) ||
   383→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.AfterAgent) ||
   384→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeModel) ||
   385→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.AfterModel) ||
   386→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeToolSelection) ||
   387→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeTool) ||
   388→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.AfterTool) ||
   389→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.PreCompress) ||
   390→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.Notification)
   391→}
   392→
   393→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Gemini CLI supports.
   394→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   395→	return []agent.HookType{
   396→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   397→		agent.HookSessionEnd,       // Maps to Gemini's SessionEnd (explicit exit/logout)
   398→		agent.HookStop,             // Maps to Gemini's AfterAgent (end of response)
   399→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, // Maps to Gemini's BeforeAgent (user prompt)
   400→		agent.HookPreToolUse,       // Maps to Gemini's BeforeTool
   401→		agent.HookPostToolUse,      // Maps to Gemini's AfterTool
   402→	}
   403→}
   404→
   405→// Helper functions for hook management
   406→
   407→// addGeminiHook adds a hook entry to matchers.
   408→// Unlike Claude Code, Gemini hooks require a "name" field.
   409→func addGeminiHook(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher, matcherName, hookName, command string) []GeminiHookMatcher {
   410→	entry := GeminiHookEntry{
   411→		Name:    hookName,
   412→		Type:    "command",
   413→		Command: command,
   414→	}
   415→
   416→	// Find or create matcher
   417→	for i, matcher := range matchers {
   418→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   419→			matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   420→			return matchers
   421→		}
   422→	}
   423→
   424→	// Create new matcher
   425→	newMatcher := GeminiHookMatcher{
   426→		Hooks: []GeminiHookEntry{entry},
   427→	}
   428→	if matcherName != "" {
   429→		newMatcher.Matcher = matcherName
   430→	}
   431→	return append(matchers, newMatcher)
   432→}
   433→
   434→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook
   435→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   436→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   437→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   438→			return true
   439→		}
   440→	}
   441→	return false
   442→}
   443→
   444→// hasEntireHook checks if any hook in the matchers is an Entire hook
   445→func hasEntireHook(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) bool {
   446→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   447→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   448→			if isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   449→				return true
   450→			}
   451→		}
   452→	}
   453→	return false
   454→}
   455→
   456→// getFirstEntireHookCommand returns the command of the first Entire hook found, or empty string
   457→func getFirstEntireHookCommand(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) string {
   458→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   459→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   460→			if isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   461→				return hook.Command
   462→			}
   463→		}
   464→	}
   465→	return ""
   466→}
   467→
   468→// removeEntireHooks removes all Entire hooks from a list of matchers
   469→func removeEntireHooks(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) []GeminiHookMatcher {
   470→	result := make([]GeminiHookMatcher, 0, len(matchers))
   471→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   472→		filteredHooks := make([]GeminiHookEntry, 0, len(matcher.Hooks))
   473→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   474→			if !isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   475→				filteredHooks = append(filteredHooks, hook)
   476→			}
   477→		}
   478→		// Only keep the matcher if it has hooks remaining
   479→		if len(filteredHooks) > 0 {
   480→			matcher.Hooks = filteredHooks
   481→			result = append(result, matcher)
   482→		}
   483→	}
   484→	return result
   485→}
   486→

   275→func TestParseHookEvent_AllHookTypes(t *testing.T) {
   276→	t.Parallel()
   277→
   278→	testCases := []struct {
   279→		hookName      string
   280→		expectedType  agent.EventType
   281→		expectNil     bool
   282→		inputTemplate string
   283→	}{
   284→		{
   285→			hookName:      HookNameSessionStart,
   286→			expectedType:  agent.SessionStart,
   287→			inputTemplate: `{"session_id": "s1", "transcript_path": "/t"}`,
   288→		},
   289→		{
   290→			hookName:      HookNameUserPromptSubmit,
   291→			expectedType:  agent.TurnStart,
   292→			inputTemplate: `{"session_id": "s2", "transcript_path": "/t", "prompt": "hi"}`,
   293→		},
   294→		{
   295→			hookName:      HookNameStop,
   296→			expectedType:  agent.TurnEnd,
   297→			inputTemplate: `{"session_id": "s3", "transcript_path": "/t"}`,
   298→		},
   299→		{
   300→			hookName:      HookNameSessionEnd,
   301→			expectedType:  agent.SessionEnd,
   302→			inputTemplate: `{"session_id": "s4", "transcript_path": "/t"}`,
   303→		},
   304→		{
   305→			hookName:      HookNamePreTask,
   306→			expectedType:  agent.SubagentStart,
   307→			inputTemplate: `{"session_id": "s5", "transcript_path": "/t", "tool_use_id": "t1", "tool_input": {}}`,
   308→		},
   309→		{
   310→			hookName:      HookNamePostTask,
   311→			expectedType:  agent.SubagentEnd,
   312→			inputTemplate: `{"session_id": "s6", "transcript_path": "/t", "tool_use_id": "t2", "tool_input": {}, "tool_response": {}}`,
   313→		},
   314→		{
   315→			hookName:      HookNamePostTodo,
   316→			expectNil:     true,
   317→			inputTemplate: `{"session_id": "s7", "transcript_path": "/t"}`,
   318→		},
   319→	}
   320→
   321→	for _, tc := range testCases {
   322→		t.Run(tc.hookName, func(t *testing.T) {
   323→			t.Parallel()
   324→
   325→			ag := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   326→			event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(tc.hookName, strings.NewReader(tc.inputTemplate))
   327→
   328→			if err != nil {
   329→				t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   330→			}
   331→
   332→			if tc.expectNil {
   333→				if event != nil {
   334→					t.Errorf("expected nil event, got %+v", event)
   335→				}
   336→				return
   337→			}
   338→
   339→			if event == nil {
   340→				t.Fatal("expected event, got nil")
   341→			}
   342→			if event.Type != tc.expectedType {
   343→				t.Errorf("expected event type %v, got %v", tc.expectedType, event.Type)
   344→			}
   345→		})
   346→	}
