```
     1→# Agent Implementation Guide
     2→
     3→## Architecture Overview
     4→
     5→The Entire CLI uses **inversion of control** for agent integration: agents are passive data providers that translate their native hook payloads into normalized lifecycle events, and the framework handles all orchestration (state transitions, file detection, checkpoint saving, metadata generation). The flow is:
     6→
     7→```
     8→Agent hook invocation → ParseHookEvent() → Event → DispatchLifecycleEvent() → framework actions
     9→```
    10→
    11→An agent never calls strategy methods or manages session state directly. It only answers questions: "What event just happened?" and "What does the transcript say?"
    12→
    13→## Quick Reference
    14→
    15→### Core Interface (`Agent`)
    16→
    17→Every agent must implement all 19 methods on the `Agent` interface:
    18→
    19→| Group | Method | Purpose |
    20→|-------|--------|---------|
    21→| **Identity** | `Name()` | Registry key (e.g., `"claude-code"`) |
    22→| | `Type()` | Display name for metadata (e.g., `"Claude Code"`) |
    23→| | `Description()` | Human-readable description for UI |
    24→| | `DetectPresence()` | Check if agent is configured in the repo |
    25→| | `ProtectedDirs()` | Directories to preserve during rewind |
    26→| **Event Mapping** | `HookNames()` | Hook verbs that become CLI subcommands |
    27→| | `ParseHookEvent()` | **Core contribution surface** - translate native hooks to Events |
    28→| **Transcript** | `ReadTranscript()` | Read raw transcript bytes |
    29→| | `ChunkTranscript()` | Split large transcripts at format-aware boundaries |
    30→| | `ReassembleTranscript()` | Recombine chunks into a single transcript |
    31→| **Session Management** | `GetHookConfigPath()` | Path to hook config file |
    32→| | `SupportsHooks()` | Whether agent supports lifecycle hooks |
    33→| | `ParseHookInput()` | Parse hook callback input from stdin |
    34→| | `GetSessionID()` | Extract session ID from hook input |
    35→| | `GetSessionDir()` | Where agent stores session data |
    36→| | `ResolveSessionFile()` | Path to session transcript file |
    37→| | `ReadSession()` | Read session data from agent's storage |
    38→| | `WriteSession()` | Write session data for resumption |
    39→| | `FormatResumeCommand()` | Command to resume a session |
    40→
    41→### Optional Interfaces
    42→
    43→| Interface | Methods | When to implement |
    44→|-----------|---------|-------------------|
    45→| `HookSupport` | `InstallHooks`, `UninstallHooks`, `AreHooksInstalled`, `GetSupportedHooks` | Agent uses a config file for hook registration (e.g., `settings.json`) |
    46→| `HookHandler` | `GetHookNames` | Backward compat - delegates to `HookNames()` |
    47→| `TranscriptAnalyzer` | `GetTranscriptPosition`, `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset`, `ExtractPrompts`, `ExtractSummary` | You want richer checkpoints with transcript-derived file lists and prompts |
    48→| `TranscriptPreparer` | `PrepareTranscript` | Agent writes transcripts asynchronously and needs a flush/sync step |
    49→| `TokenCalculator` | `CalculateTokenUsage` | Agent's transcript contains token usage data |
    50→| `SubagentAwareExtractor` | `ExtractAllModifiedFiles`, `CalculateTotalTokenUsage` | Agent spawns subagents (like Claude Code's Task tool) |
    51→| `FileWatcher` | `GetWatchPaths`, `OnFileChange` | Agent doesn't support hooks; uses file-based detection instead |
    52→
    53→## Step-by-Step Implementation Guide
    54→
    55→### Step 1: Create Package
    56→
    57→Create a new directory under `cmd/entire/cli/agent/`:
    58→
    59→```
    60→cmd/entire/cli/agent/youragent/
    61→├── youragent.go          # Core Agent implementation + init()
    62→├── lifecycle.go          # ParseHookEvent + compile-time assertions
    63→├── types.go              # Hook input structs, transcript types, tool constants
    64→├── hooks.go              # HookSupport implementation (if applicable)
    65→├── transcript.go         # TranscriptAnalyzer implementation (if applicable)
    66→├── lifecycle_test.go     # Tests for ParseHookEvent
    67→├── hooks_test.go         # Tests for hook installation
    68→└── transcript_test.go    # Tests for transcript analysis
    69→```
    70→
    71→### Step 2: Define Types (`types.go`)
    72→
    73→Define structs matching your agent's native hook JSON payloads:
    74→
    75→```go
    76→package youragent
    77→
    78→import "encoding/json"
    79→
    80→// Settings file structure (for HookSupport)
    81→type YourAgentSettings struct {
    82→    Hooks YourAgentHooks `json:"hooks"`
    83→}
    84→
    85→type YourAgentHooks struct {
    86→    SessionStart []HookMatcher `json:"SessionStart,omitempty"`
    87→    SessionEnd   []HookMatcher `json:"SessionEnd,omitempty"`
    88→    // ... other hook types your agent supports
    89→}
    90→
    91→type HookMatcher struct {
    92→    Matcher string      `json:"matcher,omitempty"`
    93→    Hooks   []HookEntry `json:"hooks"`
    94→}
    95→
    96→type HookEntry struct {
    97→    Type    string `json:"type"`
    98→    Command string `json:"command"`
    99→}
   100→
   101→// Hook input structs - match your agent's JSON payloads
   102→
   103→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
   104→    SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   105→    TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
   106→}
   107→
   108→type promptInputRaw struct {
   109→    SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   110→    TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
   111→    Prompt         string `json:"prompt"`
   112→}
   113→
   114→// Tool constants - tools in your agent that modify files
   115→const (
   116→    ToolWrite = "write_file"
   117→    ToolEdit  = "edit_file"
   118→)
   119→
   120→var FileModificationTools = []string{ToolWrite, ToolEdit}
   121→```
   122→
   123→### Step 3: Implement Core Agent Interface (`youragent.go`)
   124→
   125→```go
   126→package youragent
   127→
   128→import (
   129→    "errors"
   130→    "fmt"
   131→    "io"
   132→    "os"
   133→    "path/filepath"
   134→    "time"
   135→
   136→    "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   137→    "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
   138→)
   139→
   140→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
   141→func init() {
   142→    agent.Register("your-agent", NewYourAgent)
   143→}
   144→
   145→type YourAgent struct{}
   146→
   147→func NewYourAgent() agent.Agent {
   148→    return &YourAgent{}
   149→}
   150→
   151→// --- Identity ---
   152→
   153→func (a *YourAgent) Name() agent.AgentName    { return "your-agent" }
   154→func (a *YourAgent) Type() agent.AgentType     { return "Your Agent" }
   155→func (a *YourAgent) Description() string       { return "Your Agent - description here" }
   156→func (a *YourAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string   { return []string{".youragent"} }
   157→
   158→func (a *YourAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
   159→    repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   160→    if err != nil {
   161→        repoRoot = "."
   162→    }
   163→    _, err = os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".youragent"))
   164→    if err == nil {
   165→        return true, nil
   166→    }
   167→    return false, nil
   168→}
   169→
   170→// --- Transcript Storage ---
   171→
   172→func (a *YourAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
   173→    data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef)
   174→    if err != nil {
   175→        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   176→    }
   177→    return data, nil
   178→}
   179→
   180→func (a *YourAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   181→    // Use JSONL chunking for line-based formats
   182→    return agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   183→    // Or implement format-specific chunking (see geminicli for JSON example)
   184→}
   185→
   186→func (a *YourAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   187→    return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   188→}
   189→
   190→// --- Session Management ---
   191→
   192→func (a *YourAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string                     { return ".youragent/settings.json" }
   193→func (a *YourAgent) SupportsHooks() bool                           { return true }
   194→func (a *YourAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string    { return input.SessionID }
   195→func (a *YourAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string   { return "youragent --resume " + sessionID }
   196→
   197→func (a *YourAgent) ParseHookInput(_ agent.HookType, _ io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
   198→    return nil, errors.New("use ParseHookEvent instead")
   199→}
   200→
   201→func (a *YourAgent) GetSessionDir(_ string) (string, error) {
   202→    return "", errors.New("not implemented")
   203→}
   204→
   205→func (a *YourAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   206→    return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")
   207→}
   208→
   209→func (a *YourAgent) ReadSession(_ *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   210→    return nil, errors.New("not implemented")
   211→}
   212→
   213→func (a *YourAgent) WriteSession(_ *agent.AgentSession) error {
   214→    return errors.New("not implemented")
   215→}
   216→```
   217→
   218→### Step 4: Implement `ParseHookEvent` (`lifecycle.go`)
   219→
   220→This is the **main contribution surface** for new agents. Map each of your agent's native hook names to the normalized `EventType`:
   221→
   222→```go
   223→package youragent
   224→
   225→import (
   226→    "io"
   227→    "time"
   228→
   229→    "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   230→)
   231→
   232→// Hook name constants - these become CLI subcommands
   233→const (
   234→    HookNameSessionStart = "session-start"
   235→    HookNameSessionEnd   = "session-end"
   236→    HookNamePromptSubmit = "prompt-submit"
   237→    HookNameResponse     = "response"
   238→)
   239→
   240→func (a *YourAgent) HookNames() []string {
   241→    return []string{
   242→        HookNameSessionStart,
   243→        HookNameSessionEnd,
   244→        HookNamePromptSubmit,
   245→        HookNameResponse,
   246→    }
   247→}
   248→
   249→func (a *YourAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   250→    switch hookName {
   251→    case HookNameSessionStart:
   252→        raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   253→        if err != nil {
   254→            return nil, err
   255→        }
   256→        return &agent.Event{
   257→            Type:       agent.SessionStart,
   258→            SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   259→            SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   260→            Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   261→        }, nil
   262→
   263→    case HookNamePromptSubmit:
   264→        raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[promptInputRaw](stdin)
   265→        if err != nil {
   266→            return nil, err
   267→        }
   268→        return &agent.Event{
   269→            Type:       agent.TurnStart,
   270→            SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   271→            SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   272→            Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
   273→            Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   274→        }, nil
   275→
   276→    case HookNameResponse:
   277→        raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   278→        if err != nil {
   279→            return nil, err
   280→        }
   281→        return &agent.Event{
   282→            Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   283→            SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   284→            SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   285→            Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   286→        }, nil
   287→
   288→    case HookNameSessionEnd:
   289→        raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   290→        if err != nil {
   291→            return nil, err
   292→        }
   293→        return &agent.Event{
   294→            Type:       agent.SessionEnd,
   295→            SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   296→            SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   297→            Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   298→        }, nil
   299→
   300→    default:
   301→        // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle significance
   302→        return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil
   303→    }
   304→}
   305→```
   306→
   307→Key decisions in `ParseHookEvent`:
   308→
   309→- **Return `nil, nil`** for hooks with no lifecycle significance (pass-through hooks). This is not an error - it tells the framework to do nothing.
   310→- **Every event must have `SessionID` and `SessionRef`**. The framework validates these.
   311→- **`TurnStart` should include `Prompt`** if available - it's used for commit message generation.
   312→- **Use `agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[T]`** - the generic helper reads stdin and unmarshals JSON in one step.
   313→- **Set `Timestamp` to `time.Now()`** - the framework uses this for ordering.
   314→
   315→### Step 5: Choose and Implement Optional Interfaces
   316→
   317→See the [Optional Interface Decision Tree](#optional-interface-decision-tree) section below.
   318→
   319→### Step 6: Register via `init()`
   320→
   321→Registration happens in `init()` in your main agent file. The import side-effect pattern ensures your agent is available when the CLI starts:
   322→
   323→```go
   324→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
   325→func init() {
   326→    agent.Register("your-agent", NewYourAgent)
   327→}
   328→```
   329→
   330→Then add a blank import in the CLI's command setup to ensure the package is loaded:
   331→
   332→```go
   333→// In cmd/entire/cli/commands/ or wherever agents are imported
   334→import (
   335→    _ "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/youragent"
   336→)
   337→```
   338→
   339→### Step 7: Add Compile-Time Interface Assertions
   340→
   341→In `lifecycle.go` (or whichever file implements the optional interfaces), add compile-time checks:
   342→
   343→```go
   344→// Compile-time interface assertions
   345→var (
   346→    _ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*YourAgent)(nil)
   347→    _ agent.TokenCalculator    = (*YourAgent)(nil)
   348→    // Add one line per optional interface you implement
   349→)
   350→```
   351→
   352→Also add assertions for `HookSupport` and `HookHandler` in `hooks.go` if applicable:
   353→
   354→```go
   355→var (
   356→    _ agent.HookSupport = (*YourAgent)(nil)
   357→    _ agent.HookHandler = (*YourAgent)(nil)
   358→)
   359→```
   360→
   361→### Step 8: Implement Hook Installation (if `HookSupport`)
   362→
   363→If your agent uses a JSON config file for hooks (like Claude Code's `.claude/settings.json` or Gemini's `.gemini/settings.json`), implement `HookSupport`:
   364→
   365→```go
   366→func (a *YourAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
   367→    // 1. Find repo root
   368→    repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   369→    if err != nil {
   370→        return 0, err
   371→    }
   372→
   373→    // 2. Read existing settings (preserve unknown fields)
   374→    settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".youragent", "settings.json")
   375→    // ... read and parse ...
   376→
   377→    // 3. Build hook commands
   378→    var cmdPrefix string
   379→    if localDev {
   380→        cmdPrefix = "go run ${PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks your-agent "
   381→    } else {
   382→        cmdPrefix = "entire hooks your-agent "
   383→    }
   384→
   385→    // 4. Add hooks if they don't exist (idempotent)
   386→    // 5. Write settings back (preserving unknown fields)
   387→
   388→    return count, nil
   389→}
   390→
   391→func (a *YourAgent) UninstallHooks() error         { /* reverse of install */ }
   392→func (a *YourAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool        { /* check settings file */ }
   393→func (a *YourAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType { /* list supported types */ }
   394→```
   395→
   396→Also implement `HookHandler` for backward compatibility:
   397→
   398→```go
   399→func (a *YourAgent) GetHookNames() []string {
   400→    return a.HookNames() // delegate to the core interface method
   401→}
   402→```
   403→
   404→### Step 9: Write Tests
   405→
   406→Test `ParseHookEvent` for every hook name your agent supports. See [Testing Patterns](#testing-patterns) below.
   407→
   408→## Event Mapping Reference
   409→
   410→The framework dispatcher (`DispatchLifecycleEvent` in `lifecycle.go`) handles each event type as follows:
   411→
   412→| Event Type | Framework Actions | Claude Code Hook | Gemini CLI Hook |
   413→|------------|-------------------|------------------|-----------------|
   414→| `SessionStart` | Shows banner, checks concurrent sessions, fires state machine transition | `session-start` | `session-start` |
   415→| `TurnStart` | Captures pre-prompt state (git status, transcript position), ensures strategy setup, initializes session | `user-prompt-submit` | `before-agent` |
   416→| `TurnEnd` | Validates transcript, extracts metadata (prompts, summary, files), detects file changes via git status, saves step + checkpoint, transitions phase to IDLE | `stop` | `after-agent` |
   417→| `Compaction` | Fires compaction transition (stays ACTIVE), resets transcript offset | *(not used)* | `pre-compress` |
   418→| `SessionEnd` | Marks session as ENDED in state machine | `session-end` | `session-end` |
   419→| `SubagentStart` | Captures pre-task state (git status snapshot) | `pre-task` (PreToolUse[Task]) | *(not used)* |
   420→| `SubagentEnd` | Extracts subagent modified files, detects changes, saves task checkpoint | `post-task` (PostToolUse[Task]) | *(not used)* |
   421→
   422→### Event Field Requirements
   423→
   424→| Event Type | Required Fields | Optional Fields |
   425→|------------|----------------|-----------------|
   426→| `SessionStart` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef` | `ResponseMessage` |
   427→| `TurnStart` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef` | `Prompt`, `PreviousSessionID` |
   428→| `TurnEnd` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef` | |
   429→| `Compaction` | `SessionID` | `SessionRef` |
   430→| `SessionEnd` | `SessionID` | `SessionRef` |
   431→| `SubagentStart` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef`, `ToolUseID` | `ToolInput` |
   432→| `SubagentEnd` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef`, `ToolUseID` | `SubagentID`, `ToolInput` |
   433→
   434→## Optional Interface Decision Tree
   435→
   436→### `TranscriptAnalyzer`
   437→
   438→**What it enables:** Transcript-derived file lists (more accurate than git-status-only), extracted user prompts in checkpoint metadata, session summaries.
   439→
   440→**Without it:** The framework still creates checkpoints using git-status-based file detection and stores the raw transcript. Prompts and summary fields will be empty.
   441→
   442→**Implement when:** Your agent writes a parseable transcript (JSONL, JSON, or any structured format) and you can extract which files were modified and what the user asked.
   443→
   444→**Methods:**
   445→- `GetTranscriptPosition(path) (int, error)` - Return current position. For JSONL: line count. For JSON with messages array: message count.
   446→- `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path, startOffset) (files, currentPosition, error)` - Parse transcript from offset and return files touched by write/edit tools.
   447→- `ExtractPrompts(sessionRef, fromOffset) ([]string, error)` - Extract user prompt strings.
   448→- `ExtractSummary(sessionRef) (string, error)` - Extract last assistant response as summary.
   449→
   450→### `TranscriptPreparer`
   451→
   452→**What it enables:** A pre-read synchronization step before the framework reads the transcript.
   453→
   454→**Without it:** The framework reads the transcript immediately, which may be incomplete if the agent writes asynchronously.
   455→
   456→**Implement when:** Your agent writes transcripts asynchronously (e.g., Claude Code uses an async writer and needs to wait for a flush sentinel before reading).
   457→
   458→**Method:**
   459→- `PrepareTranscript(sessionRef) error` - Wait until the transcript is fully written. Called before `ReadTranscript`.
   460→
   461→### `TokenCalculator`
   462→
   463→**What it enables:** Token usage metrics in checkpoint metadata (input, output, cache tokens, API call count).
   464→
   465→**Without it:** Token usage fields are empty in checkpoint metadata.
   466→
   467→**Implement when:** Your agent's transcript contains token/usage data per message.
   468→
   469→**Method:**
   470→- `CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset) (*TokenUsage, error)` - Sum token usage from offset to end of transcript.
   471→
   472→### `SubagentAwareExtractor`
   473→
   474→**What it enables:** Includes files modified by spawned subagents in the checkpoint file list and aggregates subagent token usage.
   475→
   476→**Without it:** Only the main agent's transcript is analyzed. Subagent modifications are still captured by git status but not attributed to the subagent.
   477→
   478→**Implement when:** Your agent spawns subagents (task workers) that have their own transcripts.
   479→
   480→**Methods:**
   481→- `ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir) ([]string, error)` - Deduplicated file list from main + subagent transcripts.
   482→- `CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir) (*TokenUsage, error)` - Aggregated usage including subagents.
   483→
   484→### `HookSupport`
   485→
   486→**What it enables:** `entire enable` automatically installs hooks into the agent's config file.
   487→
   488→**Without it:** Users must manually configure hooks to call `entire hooks <agent> <verb>`.
   489→
   490→**Implement when:** Your agent supports a config file with hook definitions (e.g., `.claude/settings.json`, `.gemini/settings.json`).
   491→
   492→### `FileWatcher`
   493→
   494→**What it enables:** Detecting session activity by watching file changes instead of hooks.
   495→
   496→**Without it:** The agent must support hooks for the framework to receive events.
   497→
   498→**Implement when:** Your agent doesn't support lifecycle hooks but writes session data to predictable file paths.
   499→
   500→## Transcript Format Guide
   501→
   502→### JSONL Format (Claude Code pattern)
   503→
   504→One JSON object per line. Each line is a transcript entry (user message, assistant message, tool use, etc.):
   505→
   506→```
   507→{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"Fix the bug"},"timestamp":"..."}
   508→{"type":"assistant","message":{"role":"assistant","content":[...]},"timestamp":"..."}
   509→```
   510→
   511→**Chunking:** Use `agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)` - splits at newline boundaries.
   512→**Reassembly:** Use `agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks)` - concatenates with newlines.
   513→**Position:** Line count (`bufio.Reader` + count `\n`).
   514→**Offset:** Start parsing at line N (skip first N lines).
   515→
   516→### JSON Format (Gemini CLI pattern)
   517→
   518→Single JSON object with a `messages` array:
   519→
   520→```json
   521→{"messages": [{"type": "user", "content": "..."}, {"type": "gemini", "content": "..."}]}
   522→```
   523→
   524→**Chunking:** Parse the JSON, split the messages array across chunks, marshal each chunk as a complete JSON object with a subset of messages.
   525→**Reassembly:** Parse each chunk, concatenate all message arrays, marshal back.
   526→**Position:** Message count (`len(transcript.Messages)`).
   527→**Offset:** Start iterating messages at index N.
   528→
   529→### Using Chunking Helpers
   530→
   531→The `agent` package provides format-agnostic entry points:
   532→
   533→```go
   534→// These dispatch to the agent's ChunkTranscript/ReassembleTranscript methods
   535→agent.ChunkTranscript(content, agentType)     // agentType → agent lookup → format-aware chunking
   536→agent.ReassembleTranscript(chunks, agentType)  // agentType → agent lookup → format-aware reassembly
   537→
   538→// Direct JSONL helpers (usable without an agent)
   539→agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   540→agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks)
   541→
   542→// Chunk file naming
   543→agent.ChunkFileName("full.jsonl", 0)  // "full.jsonl"
   544→agent.ChunkFileName("full.jsonl", 1)  // "full.jsonl.001"
   545→agent.ParseChunkIndex("full.jsonl.002", "full.jsonl")  // 2
   546→agent.SortChunkFiles(files, "full.jsonl")  // sorted by chunk index
   547→```
   548→
   549→## Hook Installation Patterns
   550→
   551→### JSON Config File Pattern
   552→
   553→Both Claude Code and Gemini CLI use a JSON settings file in their config directory. The installation pattern is:
   554→
   555→1. **Read existing settings** as `map[string]json.RawMessage` to preserve unknown fields
   556→2. **Parse only the hook types you modify** into typed slices
   557→3. **Remove existing Entire hooks** (for `force` mode or mode-switching)
   558→4. **Add new hooks** idempotently (check if command already exists)
   559→5. **Marshal modified types back** to the raw map
   560→6. **Write the file** with pretty-printing
   561→
   562→Key principles:
   563→- **Preserve unknown fields** - don't destroy user's custom hooks or settings
   564→- **Idempotent installs** - running `entire enable` twice doesn't duplicate hooks
   565→- **Support `localDev` mode** - use `go run ${PROJECT_DIR}/...` for development
   566→- **Identify Entire hooks** by command prefix (e.g., `"entire "` or `"go run ${...}"`)
   567→
   568→### Example: Claude Code Hook Config
   569→
   570→```json
   571→{
   572→  "hooks": {
   573→    "SessionStart": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks claude-code session-start"}]}],
   574→    "Stop": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks claude-code stop"}]}],
   575→    "PreToolUse": [{"matcher": "Task", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks claude-code pre-task"}]}]
   576→  }
   577→}
   578→```
   579→
   580→### Example: Gemini CLI Hook Config
   581→
   582→```json
   583→{
   584→  "hooksConfig": {"enabled": true},
   585→  "hooks": {
   586→    "SessionStart": [{"hooks": [{"name": "entire-session-start", "type": "command", "command": "entire hooks gemini session-start"}]}],
   587→    "AfterAgent": [{"hooks": [{"name": "entire-after-agent", "type": "command", "command": "entire hooks gemini after-agent"}]}]
   588→  }
   589→}
   590→```
   591→
   592→Note: Gemini CLI requires `hooksConfig.enabled: true` and each hook entry requires a `name` field.
   593→
   594→## Testing Patterns
   595→
   596→### Testing `ParseHookEvent`
   597→
   598→Test every hook name, including pass-through hooks that return nil:
   599→
   600→```go
   601→func TestParseHookEvent_TurnStart(t *testing.T) {
   602→    t.Parallel()
   603→
   604→    ag := &YourAgent{}
   605→    input := `{"session_id": "sess-123", "transcript_path": "/tmp/t.jsonl", "prompt": "Fix the bug"}`
   606→
   607→    event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(HookNamePromptSubmit, strings.NewReader(input))
   608→
   609→    if err != nil {
   610→        t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   611→    }
   612→    if event == nil {
   613→        t.Fatal("expected event, got nil")
   614→    }
   615→    if event.Type != agent.TurnStart {
   616→        t.Errorf("expected TurnStart, got %v", event.Type)
   617→    }
   618→    if event.Prompt != "Fix the bug" {
   619→        t.Errorf("expected prompt 'Fix the bug', got %q", event.Prompt)
   620→    }
   621→}
   622→```
   623→
   624→### Testing Nil Returns
   625→
   626→Hooks with no lifecycle action must return `nil, nil`:
   627→
   628→```go
   629→func TestParseHookEvent_PassThrough_ReturnsNil(t *testing.T) {
   630→    t.Parallel()
   631→
   632→    ag := &YourAgent{}
   633→    event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent("some-pass-through-hook", strings.NewReader(`{}`))
   634→
   635→    if err != nil {
   636→        t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   637→    }
   638→    if event != nil {
   639→        t.Errorf("expected nil event, got %+v", event)
   640→    }
   641→}
   642→```
   643→
   644→### Testing Error Cases
   645→
   646→```go
   647→func TestParseHookEvent_EmptyInput(t *testing.T) {
   648→    t.Parallel()
   649→
   650→    ag := &YourAgent{}
   651→    _, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(HookNameSessionStart, strings.NewReader(""))
   652→
   653→    if err == nil {
   654→        t.Fatal("expected error for empty input")
   655→    }
   656→    if !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "empty hook input") {
   657→        t.Errorf("expected 'empty hook input' error, got: %v", err)
   658→    }
   659→}
   660→
   661→func TestParseHookEvent_MalformedJSON(t *testing.T) {
   662→    t.Parallel()
   663→
   664→    ag := &YourAgent{}
   665→    _, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(HookNameSessionStart, strings.NewReader("not json"))
   666→
   667→    if err == nil {
   668→        t.Fatal("expected error for malformed JSON")
   669→    }
   670→}
   671→```
   672→
   673→### Testing Hook Installation
   674→
   675→```go
   676→func TestInstallHooks_CreatesSettingsFile(t *testing.T) {
   677→    t.Parallel()
   678→
   679→    dir := t.TempDir()
   680→    // ... set up test repo, create .youragent/ directory ...
   681→
   682→    ag := &YourAgent{}
   683→    count, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)
   684→
   685→    if err != nil {
   686→        t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   687→    }
   688→    if count == 0 {
   689→        t.Error("expected hooks to be installed")
   690→    }
   691→
   692→    // Verify settings file was created and contains hooks
   693→    data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(dir, ".youragent", "settings.json"))
   694→    // ... assert hooks are present ...
   695→}
   696→
   697→func TestInstallHooks_Idempotent(t *testing.T) {
   698→    t.Parallel()
   699→
   700→    // Install twice, second call should return count=0
   701→    ag := &YourAgent{}
   702→    ag.InstallHooks(false, false)
   703→    count, _ := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)
   704→    if count != 0 {
   705→        t.Errorf("expected 0 new hooks on second install, got %d", count)
   706→    }
   707→}
   708→```
   709→
   710→### Test file references
   711→
   712→- Claude Code lifecycle tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/lifecycle_test.go`
   713→- Claude Code hooks tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/hooks_test.go`
   714→- Claude Code transcript tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/transcript_test.go`
   715→- Gemini CLI lifecycle tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/lifecycle_test.go`
   716→- Gemini CLI hooks tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/hooks_test.go`
   717→- Gemini CLI transcript tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript_test.go`
   718→
   719→## Common Pitfalls
   720→
   721→### go-git v5 Bugs
   722→
   723→**Do NOT use go-git v5 for `checkout` or `reset --hard` operations.** go-git v5 has a bug where `worktree.Reset()` with `HardReset` and `worktree.Checkout()` incorrectly delete untracked directories even when listed in `.gitignore`. This would destroy `.entire/` and agent config directories. Use the git CLI instead. See `CLAUDE.md` for details and `hard_reset_test.go` for regression tests.
   724→
   725→### Repo Root vs Current Working Directory
   726→
   727→Git commands return paths relative to the **repository root**, not the current working directory. When your code runs from a subdirectory, `os.Getwd()` gives wrong results for path construction. Always use `paths.RepoRoot()`:
   728→
   729→```go
   730→// WRONG
   731→cwd, _ := os.Getwd()
   732→absPath := filepath.Join(cwd, file) // Breaks from subdirectory
   733→
   734→// CORRECT
   735→repoRoot, _ := paths.RepoRoot()
   736→absPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, file)
   737→```
   738→
   739→### Transcript Flush Timing
   740→
   741→Some agents write transcripts asynchronously. If `ReadTranscript` is called before the write completes, the transcript will be incomplete. Implement `TranscriptPreparer` if your agent has this behavior. Claude Code solves this by writing a sentinel entry and polling for it (see `waitForTranscriptFlush` in `claudecode/lifecycle.go`).
   742→
   743→### Nil Event Return Pattern
   744→
   745→`ParseHookEvent` returning `(nil, nil)` is **not an error** - it means the hook has no lifecycle significance. The framework (in `hook_registry.go`) checks:
   746→
   747→```go
   748→event, parseErr := ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, stdin)
   749→if event != nil {
   750→    hookErr = DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)
   751→}
   752→// nil event → no-op (hook is silently acknowledged)
   753→```
   754→
   755→Use `//nolint:nilnil` to suppress the linter warning on intentional nil returns.
   756→
   757→### Agent Name vs Agent Type
   758→
   759→- `AgentName` is the **registry key** used in code (`"claude-code"`, `"gemini"`). It appears in CLI commands: `entire hooks claude-code stop`.
   760→- `AgentType` is the **display name** stored in metadata and commit trailers (`"Claude Code"`, `"Gemini CLI"`). It's what users see.
   761→
   762→Register constants for both in `cmd/entire/cli/agent/registry.go` when adding a new agent.
   763→
   764→### Hook Names as CLI Subcommands
   765→
   766→The strings returned by `HookNames()` become literal CLI subcommands under `entire hooks <agent>`. For example, if `HookNames()` returns `["session-start", "stop"]`, the CLI creates:
   767→- `entire hooks your-agent session-start`
   768→- `entire hooks your-agent stop`
   769→
   770→These commands read JSON from stdin and dispatch to `ParseHookEvent`. The agent's hook config should invoke these commands.
   771→
   772→## Complete Code Template
   773→
   774→A minimal but functional agent skeleton. Copy this directory structure and fill in agent-specific details:
   775→
   776→<details>
   777→<summary>youragent/types.go</summary>
   778→
   779→```go
   780→package youragent
   781→
   782→// sessionInfoRaw matches your agent's session hook JSON payload.
   783→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
   784→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   785→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
   786→}
   787→
   788→// promptInputRaw matches your agent's prompt-submit hook JSON payload.
   789→type promptInputRaw struct {
   790→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   791→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
   792→	Prompt         string `json:"prompt"`
   793→}
   794→```
   795→
   796→</details>
   797→
   798→<details>
   799→<summary>youragent/youragent.go</summary>
   800→
   801→```go
   802→package youragent
   803→
   804→import (
   805→	"errors"
   806→	"fmt"
   807→	"io"
   808→	"os"
   809→	"path/filepath"
   810→
   811→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   812→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
   813→)
   814→
   815→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
   816→func init() {
   817→	agent.Register("your-agent", NewYourAgent)
   818→}
   819→
   820→type YourAgent struct{}
   821→
   822→func NewYourAgent() agent.Agent { return &YourAgent{} }
   823→
   824→func (a *YourAgent) Name() agent.AgentName  { return "your-agent" }
   825→func (a *YourAgent) Type() agent.AgentType   { return "Your Agent" }
   826→func (a *YourAgent) Description() string     { return "Your Agent - brief description" }
   827→func (a *YourAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".youragent"} }
   828→
   829→func (a *YourAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
   830→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   831→	if err != nil {
   832→		repoRoot = "."
   833→	}
   834→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".youragent")); err == nil {
   835→		return true, nil
   836→	}
   837→	return false, nil
   838→}
   839→
   840→func (a *YourAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
   841→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path from agent hook
   842→	if err != nil {
   843→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   844→	}
   845→	return data, nil
   846→}
   847→
   848→func (a *YourAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   849→	return agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   850→}
   851→
   852→func (a *YourAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   853→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   854→}
   855→
   856→// --- Session Management ---
   857→func (a *YourAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string                                          { return "" }
   858→func (a *YourAgent) SupportsHooks() bool                                                { return true }
   859→func (a *YourAgent) ParseHookInput(_ agent.HookType, _ io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) { return nil, errors.New("use ParseHookEvent") }
   860→func (a *YourAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string                         { return input.SessionID }
   861→func (a *YourAgent) GetSessionDir(_ string) (string, error)                             { return "", errors.New("not implemented") }
   862→func (a *YourAgent) ResolveSessionFile(dir, id string) string                           { return filepath.Join(dir, id+".jsonl") }
   863→func (a *YourAgent) ReadSession(_ *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error)        { return nil, errors.New("not implemented") }
   864→func (a *YourAgent) WriteSession(_ *agent.AgentSession) error                           { return errors.New("not implemented") }
   865→func (a *YourAgent) FormatResumeCommand(id string) string                               { return "youragent --resume " + id }

   871→<summary>youragent/lifecycle.go</summary>
   872→
   873→```go
   874→package youragent
   875→
   876→import (
   877→	"io"
   878→	"time"
   879→
   880→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   881→)
   882→
   883→const (
   884→	HookNameSessionStart = "session-start"
   885→	HookNameSessionEnd   = "session-end"
   886→	HookNamePromptSubmit = "prompt-submit"
   887→	HookNameResponse     = "response"
   888→)
   889→
   890→func (a *YourAgent) HookNames() []string {
   891→	return []string{
   892→		HookNameSessionStart,
   893→		HookNameSessionEnd,
   894→		HookNamePromptSubmit,
   895→		HookNameResponse,
   896→	}
   897→}
   898→
   899→func (a *YourAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   900→	switch hookName {
   901→	case HookNameSessionStart:
   902→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   903→		if err != nil {
   904→			return nil, err
   905→		}
   906→		return &agent.Event{
   907→			Type:       agent.SessionStart,
   908→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   909→			SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   910→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   911→		}, nil
   912→
   913→	case HookNamePromptSubmit:
   914→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[promptInputRaw](stdin)
   915→		if err != nil {
   916→			return nil, err
   917→		}
   918→		return &agent.Event{
   919→			Type:       agent.TurnStart,
   920→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   921→			SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   922→			Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
   923→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   924→		}, nil
   925→
   926→	case HookNameResponse:
   927→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   928→		if err != nil {
   929→			return nil, err
   930→		}
   931→		return &agent.Event{
   932→			Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   933→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   934→			SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   935→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   936→		}, nil
   937→
   938→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
   939→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   940→		if err != nil {
   941→			return nil, err
   942→		}
   943→		return &agent.Event{
   944→			Type:       agent.SessionEnd,
   945→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   946→			SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   947→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   948→		}, nil
   949→
   950→	default:
   951→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
   952→	}
   953→}
   954→```

## PR Summary

<!-- CURSOR_SUMMARY -->
**Low Risk**
Documentation-only change with no runtime or behavioral impact on the CLI.

**Overview**
Adds a new `docs/architecture/agent-guide.md` that documents how to integrate new agents into the Entire CLI, including the required `Agent` interface methods, how to map native hooks via `ParseHookEvent`, and how to register agents.

The guide also covers optional interfaces (hooks installation, transcript analysis, token usage, subagent extraction, file watching), recommended hook-install patterns, transcript chunking/reassembly guidance, common pitfalls, and example code + test templates for a minimal agent skeleton.

<sup>Written by [Cursor Bugbot](https://cursor.com/dashboard?tab=bugbot) for commit b872ee4c75e57315efb9fc80a267408e88a49f66. Configure [here](https://cursor.com/dashboard?tab=bugbot).</sup>
<!-- /CURSOR_SUMMARY -->
## Pull request overview

Adds a new architecture guide documenting how to implement and integrate new AI agents into Entire CLI’s agent abstraction layer (hooks → normalized lifecycle events → framework orchestration), intended to be usable by both humans and agents.

**Changes:**
- Introduces a comprehensive “Agent Implementation Guide” covering required `agent.Agent` methods, optional interfaces, and event mapping.
- Provides implementation templates for an example agent package (types, lifecycle parsing, hook installation patterns, and tests).
- Documents transcript formats (JSONL/JSON), chunking helpers, and common pitfalls (repo root handling, go-git caveats, nil-event behavior).

File: docs/architecture/agent-guide.md:310
Author: Copilot
Body: This bullet says every event must include both `SessionID` and `SessionRef`, but later in the same document you list `SessionRef` as optional for `SessionEnd` (and the framework’s `handleLifecycleSessionEnd` only requires `SessionID`). Please reconcile this guidance—ideally point readers to the per-EventType requirements table rather than stating a blanket rule.
```suggestion
- **Every event must include the required identifiers for its `EventType`** (see the per-EventType requirements table). `SessionID` is always required; `SessionRef` is required only where marked and is otherwise optional but recommended.
```

File: docs/architecture/agent-guide.md:384
Author: Copilot
Body: The hook installation example uses `go run ${PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go ...`, but the existing agents use agent-specific env vars (`${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}`, `${GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR}`) and detect Entire-installed hooks via those prefixes. For consistency (and to avoid suggesting a non-existent env var), please align this example with the established pattern or explicitly call out that `${PROJECT_DIR}` is a placeholder that must match the agent’s actual environment variable and `entireHookPrefixes` logic.

---
File: docs/architecture/agent-guide.md:46
Author: Copilot
Body: `HookHandler` is described as optional/backward-compat here, but in the current CLI it is effectively required for hook subcommand registration: `cmd/entire/cli/hook_registry.go` only creates `entire hooks <agent> <verb>` commands by iterating `handler.GetHookNames()` on agents that implement `agent.HookHandler`. Please update this section to clearly state that new agents must implement `HookHandler` (at least for now) or their hooks won’t be callable via the CLI.
```suggestion
| `HookHandler` | `GetHookNames` | **Currently required for CLI hook subcommand registration** – new agents MUST implement this or their hooks won't be callable via `entire hooks` (planned to be folded into `Agent` / truly optional in a future CLI revision) |
```

---
File: docs/architecture/agent-guide.md:198
Author: Copilot
Body: The sample `ParseHookInput` implementation returns an error telling readers to use `ParseHookEvent`. However `ParseHookInput` is still part of the required `agent.Agent` interface and is exercised by integration tests (e.g. `cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/agent_strategy_test.go`). Please adjust the guide to either (a) show a real `ParseHookInput` implementation (like `claudecode` / `geminicli`), or (b) explain precisely when it is safe to stub this out.
```suggestion
// ParseHookInput is used by the legacy hook entrypoint and some integration tests.
// You must provide a real implementation, even if your primary path uses ParseHookEvent.
func (a *YourAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, r io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    // Minimal example: read the raw payload and construct a HookInput.
    // In a real agent, you would typically also extract a session ID and other metadata here.
    body, err := io.ReadAll(r)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("read hook payload: %w", err)
    }

    return &agent.HookInput{
        // Fill in the fields defined on agent.HookInput for your agent.
        // For example (field names may differ):
        // Type:    hookType,
        // RawBody: body,
        // SessionID: extractSessionID(body),
    }, nil
```

```
     1→# Agent Implementation Guide
     2→
     3→## Architecture Overview
     4→
     5→The Entire CLI uses **inversion of control** for agent integration: agents are passive data providers that translate their native hook payloads into normalized lifecycle events, and the framework handles all orchestration (state transitions, file detection, checkpoint saving, metadata generation). The flow is:
     6→
     7→```
     8→Agent hook invocation → ParseHookEvent() → Event → DispatchLifecycleEvent() → framework actions
     9→```
    10→
    11→An agent never calls strategy methods or manages session state directly. It only answers questions: "What event just happened?" and "What does the transcript say?"
    12→
    13→## Quick Reference
    14→
    15→### Core Interface (`Agent`)
    16→
    17→Every agent must implement all 19 methods on the `Agent` interface:
    18→
    19→| Group | Method | Purpose |
    20→|-------|--------|---------|
    21→| **Identity** | `Name()` | Registry key (e.g., `"claude-code"`) |
    22→| | `Type()` | Display name for metadata (e.g., `"Claude Code"`) |
    23→| | `Description()` | Human-readable description for UI |
    24→| | `DetectPresence()` | Check if agent is configured in the repo |
    25→| | `ProtectedDirs()` | Directories to preserve during rewind |
    26→| **Event Mapping** | `HookNames()` | Hook verbs that become CLI subcommands |
    27→| | `ParseHookEvent()` | **Core contribution surface** - translate native hooks to Events |
    28→| **Transcript** | `ReadTranscript()` | Read raw transcript bytes |
    29→| | `ChunkTranscript()` | Split large transcripts at format-aware boundaries |
    30→| | `ReassembleTranscript()` | Recombine chunks into a single transcript |
    31→| **Session Management** | `GetHookConfigPath()` | Path to hook config file |
    32→| | `SupportsHooks()` | Whether agent supports lifecycle hooks |
    33→| | `ParseHookInput()` | Parse hook callback input from stdin |
    34→| | `GetSessionID()` | Extract session ID from hook input |
    35→| | `GetSessionDir()` | Where agent stores session data |
    36→| | `ResolveSessionFile()` | Path to session transcript file |
    37→| | `ReadSession()` | Read session data from agent's storage |
    38→| | `WriteSession()` | Write session data for resumption |
    39→| | `FormatResumeCommand()` | Command to resume a session |
    40→
    41→### Optional Interfaces
    42→
    43→| Interface | Methods | When to implement |
    44→|-----------|---------|-------------------|
    45→| `HookSupport` | `InstallHooks`, `UninstallHooks`, `AreHooksInstalled`, `GetSupportedHooks` | Agent uses a config file for hook registration (e.g., `settings.json`) |
    46→| `HookHandler` | `GetHookNames` | Backward compat - delegates to `HookNames()` |
    47→| `TranscriptAnalyzer` | `GetTranscriptPosition`, `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset`, `ExtractPrompts`, `ExtractSummary` | You want richer checkpoints with transcript-derived file lists and prompts |
    48→| `TranscriptPreparer` | `PrepareTranscript` | Agent writes transcripts asynchronously and needs a flush/sync step |
    49→| `TokenCalculator` | `CalculateTokenUsage` | Agent's transcript contains token usage data |
    50→| `SubagentAwareExtractor` | `ExtractAllModifiedFiles`, `CalculateTotalTokenUsage` | Agent spawns subagents (like Claude Code's Task tool) |
    51→| `FileWatcher` | `GetWatchPaths`, `OnFileChange` | Agent doesn't support hooks; uses file-based detection instead |
    52→
    53→## Step-by-Step Implementation Guide
    54→
    55→### Step 1: Create Package
    56→
    57→Create a new directory under `cmd/entire/cli/agent/`:
    58→
    59→```
    60→cmd/entire/cli/agent/youragent/
    61→├── youragent.go          # Core Agent implementation + init()
    62→├── lifecycle.go          # ParseHookEvent + compile-time assertions
    63→├── types.go              # Hook input structs, transcript types, tool constants
    64→├── hooks.go              # HookSupport implementation (if applicable)
    65→├── transcript.go         # TranscriptAnalyzer implementation (if applicable)
    66→├── lifecycle_test.go     # Tests for ParseHookEvent
    67→├── hooks_test.go         # Tests for hook installation
    68→└── transcript_test.go    # Tests for transcript analysis
    69→```
    70→
    71→### Step 2: Define Types (`types.go`)
    72→
    73→Define structs matching your agent's native hook JSON payloads:
    74→
    75→```go
    76→package youragent
    77→
    78→// Settings file structure (for HookSupport)
    79→type YourAgentSettings struct {
    80→    Hooks YourAgentHooks `json:"hooks"`
    81→}
    82→
    83→type YourAgentHooks struct {
    84→    SessionStart []HookMatcher `json:"SessionStart,omitempty"`
    85→    SessionEnd   []HookMatcher `json:"SessionEnd,omitempty"`
    86→    // ... other hook types your agent supports
    87→}
    88→
    89→type HookMatcher struct {
    90→    Matcher string      `json:"matcher,omitempty"`
    91→    Hooks   []HookEntry `json:"hooks"`
    92→}
    93→
    94→type HookEntry struct {
    95→    Type    string `json:"type"`
    96→    Command string `json:"command"`
    97→}
    98→
    99→// Hook input structs - match your agent's JSON payloads
   100→
   101→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
   102→    SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   103→    TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
   104→}
   105→
   106→type promptInputRaw struct {
   107→    SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   108→    TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
   109→    Prompt         string `json:"prompt"`
   110→}
   111→
   112→// Tool constants - tools in your agent that modify files
   113→const (
   114→    ToolWrite = "write_file"
   115→    ToolEdit  = "edit_file"
   116→)
   117→
   118→var FileModificationTools = []string{ToolWrite, ToolEdit}
   119→```
   120→
   121→### Step 3: Implement Core Agent Interface (`youragent.go`)
   122→
   123→```go
   124→package youragent
   125→
   126→import (
   127→    "errors"
   128→    "fmt"
   129→    "io"
   130→    "os"
   131→    "path/filepath"
   132→
   133→    "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   134→    "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
   135→)
   136→
   137→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
   138→func init() {
   139→    agent.Register("your-agent", NewYourAgent)
   140→}
   141→
   142→type YourAgent struct{}
   143→
   144→func NewYourAgent() agent.Agent {
   145→    return &YourAgent{}
   146→}
   147→
   148→// --- Identity ---
   149→
   150→func (a *YourAgent) Name() agent.AgentName    { return "your-agent" }
   151→func (a *YourAgent) Type() agent.AgentType     { return "Your Agent" }
   152→func (a *YourAgent) Description() string       { return "Your Agent - description here" }
   153→func (a *YourAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string   { return []string{".youragent"} }
   154→
   155→func (a *YourAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
   156→    repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   157→    if err != nil {
   158→        repoRoot = "."
   159→    }
   160→    _, err = os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".youragent"))
   161→    if err == nil {
   162→        return true, nil
   163→    }
   164→    return false, nil
   165→}
   166→
   167→// --- Transcript Storage ---
   168→
   169→func (a *YourAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
   170→    data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef)
   171→    if err != nil {
   172→        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   173→    }
   174→    return data, nil
   175→}
   176→
   177→func (a *YourAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   178→    // Use JSONL chunking for line-based formats
   179→    return agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   180→    // Or implement format-specific chunking (see geminicli for JSON example)
   181→}
   182→
   183→func (a *YourAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   184→    return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   185→}
   186→
   187→// --- Session Management ---
   188→
   189→func (a *YourAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string                     { return ".youragent/settings.json" }
   190→func (a *YourAgent) SupportsHooks() bool                           { return true }
   191→func (a *YourAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string    { return input.SessionID }
   192→func (a *YourAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string   { return "youragent --resume " + sessionID }
   193→
   194→func (a *YourAgent) ParseHookInput(_ agent.HookType, _ io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
   195→    return nil, errors.New("use ParseHookEvent instead")
   196→}
   197→
   198→func (a *YourAgent) GetSessionDir(_ string) (string, error) {
   199→    return "", errors.New("not implemented")
   200→}
   201→
   202→func (a *YourAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   203→    return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")
   204→}
   205→
   206→func (a *YourAgent) ReadSession(_ *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   207→    return nil, errors.New("not implemented")
   208→}
   209→
   210→func (a *YourAgent) WriteSession(_ *agent.AgentSession) error {
   211→    return errors.New("not implemented")
   212→}
   213→```
   214→
   215→### Step 4: Implement `ParseHookEvent` (`lifecycle.go`)
   216→
   217→This is the **main contribution surface** for new agents. Map each of your agent's native hook names to the normalized `EventType`:
   218→
   219→```go
   220→package youragent
   221→
   222→import (
   223→    "io"
   224→    "time"
   225→
   226→    "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   227→)
   228→
   229→// Hook name constants - these become CLI subcommands
   230→const (
   231→    HookNameSessionStart = "session-start"
   232→    HookNameSessionEnd   = "session-end"
   233→    HookNamePromptSubmit = "prompt-submit"
   234→    HookNameResponse     = "response"
   235→)
   236→
   237→func (a *YourAgent) HookNames() []string {
   238→    return []string{
   239→        HookNameSessionStart,
   240→        HookNameSessionEnd,
   241→        HookNamePromptSubmit,
   242→        HookNameResponse,
   243→    }
   244→}
   245→
   246→func (a *YourAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   247→    switch hookName {
   248→    case HookNameSessionStart:
   249→        raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   250→        if err != nil {
   251→            return nil, err
   252→        }
   253→        return &agent.Event{
   254→            Type:       agent.SessionStart,
   255→            SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   256→            SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   257→            Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   258→        }, nil
   259→
   260→    case HookNamePromptSubmit:
   261→        raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[promptInputRaw](stdin)
   262→        if err != nil {
   263→            return nil, err
   264→        }
   265→        return &agent.Event{
   266→            Type:       agent.TurnStart,
   267→            SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   268→            SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   269→            Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
   270→            Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   271→        }, nil
   272→
   273→    case HookNameResponse:
   274→        raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   275→        if err != nil {
   276→            return nil, err
   277→        }
   278→        return &agent.Event{
   279→            Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   280→            SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   281→            SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   282→            Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   283→        }, nil
   284→
   285→    case HookNameSessionEnd:
   286→        raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   287→        if err != nil {
   288→            return nil, err
   289→        }
   290→        return &agent.Event{
   291→            Type:       agent.SessionEnd,
   292→            SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   293→            SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   294→            Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   295→        }, nil
   296→
   297→    default:
   298→        // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle significance
   299→        return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil
   300→    }
   301→}
   302→```
   303→
   304→Key decisions in `ParseHookEvent`:
   305→
   306→- **Return `nil, nil`** for hooks with no lifecycle significance (pass-through hooks). This is not an error - it tells the framework to do nothing.
   307→- **Every event must include the required fields for its `EventType`** (see the [Event Field Requirements](#event-field-requirements) table). `SessionID` is always required; `SessionRef` is required for most event types but optional for `Compaction` and `SessionEnd`.
   308→- **`TurnStart` should include `Prompt`** if available - it's used for commit message generation.
   309→- **Use `agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[T]`** - the generic helper reads stdin and unmarshals JSON in one step.
   310→- **Set `Timestamp` to `time.Now()`** - the framework uses this for ordering.
   311→
   312→### Step 5: Choose and Implement Optional Interfaces
   313→
   314→See the [Optional Interface Decision Tree](#optional-interface-decision-tree) section below.
   315→
   316→### Step 6: Register via `init()`
   317→
   318→Registration happens in `init()` in your main agent file. The import side-effect pattern ensures your agent is available when the CLI starts:
   319→
   320→```go
   321→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
   322→func init() {
   323→    agent.Register("your-agent", NewYourAgent)
   324→}
   325→```
   326→
   327→Then add a blank import in the CLI's command setup to ensure the package is loaded:
   328→
   329→```go
   330→// In cmd/entire/cli/commands/ or wherever agents are imported
   331→import (
   332→    _ "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/youragent"
   333→)
   334→```
   335→
   336→### Step 7: Add Compile-Time Interface Assertions
   337→
   338→In `lifecycle.go` (or whichever file implements the optional interfaces), add compile-time checks:
   339→
   340→```go
   341→// Compile-time interface assertions
   342→var (
   343→    _ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*YourAgent)(nil)
   344→    _ agent.TokenCalculator    = (*YourAgent)(nil)
   345→    // Add one line per optional interface you implement
   346→)
   347→```
   348→
   349→Also add assertions for `HookSupport` and `HookHandler` in `hooks.go` if applicable:
   350→
   351→```go
   352→var (
   353→    _ agent.HookSupport = (*YourAgent)(nil)
   354→    _ agent.HookHandler = (*YourAgent)(nil)
   355→)
   356→```
   357→
   358→### Step 8: Implement Hook Installation (if `HookSupport`)
   359→
   360→If your agent uses a JSON config file for hooks (like Claude Code's `.claude/settings.json` or Gemini's `.gemini/settings.json`), implement `HookSupport`:
   361→
   362→```go
   363→func (a *YourAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
   364→    // 1. Find repo root
   365→    repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   366→    if err != nil {
   367→        return 0, err
   368→    }
   369→
   370→    // 2. Read existing settings (preserve unknown fields)
   371→    settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".youragent", "settings.json")
   372→    // ... read and parse ...
   373→
   374→    // 3. Build hook commands
   375→    var cmdPrefix string
   376→    if localDev {
   377→        cmdPrefix = "go run ${PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks your-agent "
   378→    } else {
   379→        cmdPrefix = "entire hooks your-agent "
   380→    }
   381→
   382→    // 4. Add hooks if they don't exist (idempotent)
   383→    // 5. Write settings back (preserving unknown fields)
   384→
   385→    return count, nil
   386→}
   387→
   388→func (a *YourAgent) UninstallHooks() error         { /* reverse of install */ }
   389→func (a *YourAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool        { /* check settings file */ }
   390→func (a *YourAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType { /* list supported types */ }
   391→```
   392→
   393→Also implement `HookHandler` for backward compatibility:
   394→
   395→```go
   396→func (a *YourAgent) GetHookNames() []string {
   397→    return a.HookNames() // delegate to the core interface method
   398→}
   399→```
   400→
   401→### Step 9: Write Tests
   402→
   403→Test `ParseHookEvent` for every hook name your agent supports. See [Testing Patterns](#testing-patterns) below.
   404→
   405→## Event Mapping Reference
   406→
   407→The framework dispatcher (`DispatchLifecycleEvent` in `lifecycle.go`) handles each event type as follows:
   408→
   409→| Event Type | Framework Actions | Claude Code Hook | Gemini CLI Hook |
   410→|------------|-------------------|------------------|-----------------|
   411→| `SessionStart` | Shows banner, checks concurrent sessions, fires state machine transition | `session-start` | `session-start` |
   412→| `TurnStart` | Captures pre-prompt state (git status, transcript position), ensures strategy setup, initializes session | `user-prompt-submit` | `before-agent` |
   413→| `TurnEnd` | Validates transcript, extracts metadata (prompts, summary, files), detects file changes via git status, saves step + checkpoint, transitions phase to IDLE | `stop` | `after-agent` |
   414→| `Compaction` | Fires compaction transition (stays ACTIVE), resets transcript offset | *(not used)* | `pre-compress` |
   415→| `SessionEnd` | Marks session as ENDED in state machine | `session-end` | `session-end` |
   416→| `SubagentStart` | Captures pre-task state (git status snapshot) | `pre-task` (PreToolUse[Task]) | *(not used)* |
   417→| `SubagentEnd` | Extracts subagent modified files, detects changes, saves task checkpoint | `post-task` (PostToolUse[Task]) | *(not used)* |
   418→
   419→### Event Field Requirements
   420→
   421→| Event Type | Required Fields | Optional Fields |
   422→|------------|----------------|-----------------|
   423→| `SessionStart` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef` | `ResponseMessage` |
   424→| `TurnStart` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef` | `Prompt`, `PreviousSessionID` |
   425→| `TurnEnd` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef` | |
   426→| `Compaction` | `SessionID` | `SessionRef` |
   427→| `SessionEnd` | `SessionID` | `SessionRef` |
   428→| `SubagentStart` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef`, `ToolUseID` | `ToolInput` |
   429→| `SubagentEnd` | `SessionID`, `SessionRef`, `ToolUseID` | `SubagentID`, `ToolInput` |
   430→
   431→## Optional Interface Decision Tree
   432→
   433→### `TranscriptAnalyzer`
   434→
   435→**What it enables:** Transcript-derived file lists (more accurate than git-status-only), extracted user prompts in checkpoint metadata, session summaries.
   436→
   437→**Without it:** The framework still creates checkpoints using git-status-based file detection and stores the raw transcript. Prompts and summary fields will be empty.
   438→
   439→**Implement when:** Your agent writes a parseable transcript (JSONL, JSON, or any structured format) and you can extract which files were modified and what the user asked.
   440→
   441→**Methods:**
   442→- `GetTranscriptPosition(path) (int, error)` - Return current position. For JSONL: line count. For JSON with messages array: message count.
   443→- `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path, startOffset) (files, currentPosition, error)` - Parse transcript from offset and return files touched by write/edit tools.
   444→- `ExtractPrompts(sessionRef, fromOffset) ([]string, error)` - Extract user prompt strings.
   445→- `ExtractSummary(sessionRef) (string, error)` - Extract last assistant response as summary.
   446→
   447→### `TranscriptPreparer`
   448→
   449→**What it enables:** A pre-read synchronization step before the framework reads the transcript.
   450→
   451→**Without it:** The framework reads the transcript immediately, which may be incomplete if the agent writes asynchronously.
   452→
   453→**Implement when:** Your agent writes transcripts asynchronously (e.g., Claude Code uses an async writer and needs to wait for a flush sentinel before reading).
   454→
   455→**Method:**
   456→- `PrepareTranscript(sessionRef) error` - Wait until the transcript is fully written. Called before `ReadTranscript`.
   457→
   458→### `TokenCalculator`
   459→
   460→**What it enables:** Token usage metrics in checkpoint metadata (input, output, cache tokens, API call count).
   461→
   462→**Without it:** Token usage fields are empty in checkpoint metadata.
   463→
   464→**Implement when:** Your agent's transcript contains token/usage data per message.
   465→
   466→**Method:**
   467→- `CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset) (*TokenUsage, error)` - Sum token usage from offset to end of transcript.
   468→
   469→### `SubagentAwareExtractor`
   470→
   471→**What it enables:** Includes files modified by spawned subagents in the checkpoint file list and aggregates subagent token usage.
   472→
   473→**Without it:** Only the main agent's transcript is analyzed. Subagent modifications are still captured by git status but not attributed to the subagent.
   474→
   475→**Implement when:** Your agent spawns subagents (task workers) that have their own transcripts.
   476→
   477→**Methods:**
   478→- `ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir) ([]string, error)` - Deduplicated file list from main + subagent transcripts.
   479→- `CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir) (*TokenUsage, error)` - Aggregated usage including subagents.
   480→
   481→### `HookSupport`
   482→
   483→**What it enables:** `entire enable` automatically installs hooks into the agent's config file.
   484→
   485→**Without it:** Users must manually configure hooks to call `entire hooks <agent> <verb>`.
   486→
   487→**Implement when:** Your agent supports a config file with hook definitions (e.g., `.claude/settings.json`, `.gemini/settings.json`).
   488→
   489→### `FileWatcher`
   490→
   491→**What it enables:** Detecting session activity by watching file changes instead of hooks.
   492→
   493→**Without it:** The agent must support hooks for the framework to receive events.
   494→
   495→**Implement when:** Your agent doesn't support lifecycle hooks but writes session data to predictable file paths.
   496→
   497→## Transcript Format Guide
   498→
   499→### JSONL Format (Claude Code pattern)
   500→
   501→One JSON object per line. Each line is a transcript entry (user message, assistant message, tool use, etc.):
   502→
   503→```
   504→{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"Fix the bug"},"timestamp":"..."}
   505→{"type":"assistant","message":{"role":"assistant","content":[...]},"timestamp":"..."}
   506→```
   507→
   508→**Chunking:** Use `agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)` - splits at newline boundaries.
   509→**Reassembly:** Use `agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks)` - concatenates with newlines.
   510→**Position:** Line count (`bufio.Reader` + count `\n`).
   511→**Offset:** Start parsing at line N (skip first N lines).
   512→
   513→### JSON Format (Gemini CLI pattern)
   514→
   515→Single JSON object with a `messages` array:
   516→
   517→```json
   518→{"messages": [{"type": "user", "content": "..."}, {"type": "gemini", "content": "..."}]}
   519→```
   520→
   521→**Chunking:** Parse the JSON, split the messages array across chunks, marshal each chunk as a complete JSON object with a subset of messages.
   522→**Reassembly:** Parse each chunk, concatenate all message arrays, marshal back.
   523→**Position:** Message count (`len(transcript.Messages)`).
   524→**Offset:** Start iterating messages at index N.
   525→
   526→### Using Chunking Helpers
   527→
   528→The `agent` package provides format-agnostic entry points:
   529→
   530→```go
   531→// These dispatch to the agent's ChunkTranscript/ReassembleTranscript methods
   532→agent.ChunkTranscript(content, agentType)     // agentType → agent lookup → format-aware chunking
   533→agent.ReassembleTranscript(chunks, agentType)  // agentType → agent lookup → format-aware reassembly
   534→
   535→// Direct JSONL helpers (usable without an agent)
   536→agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   537→agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks)
   538→
   539→// Chunk file naming
   540→agent.ChunkFileName("full.jsonl", 0)  // "full.jsonl"
   541→agent.ChunkFileName("full.jsonl", 1)  // "full.jsonl.001"
   542→agent.ParseChunkIndex("full.jsonl.002", "full.jsonl")  // 2
   543→agent.SortChunkFiles(files, "full.jsonl")  // sorted by chunk index
   544→```
   545→
   546→## Hook Installation Patterns
   547→
   548→### JSON Config File Pattern
   549→
   550→Both Claude Code and Gemini CLI use a JSON settings file in their config directory. The installation pattern is:
   551→
   552→1. **Read existing settings** as `map[string]json.RawMessage` to preserve unknown fields
   553→2. **Parse only the hook types you modify** into typed slices
   554→3. **Remove existing Entire hooks** (for `force` mode or mode-switching)
   555→4. **Add new hooks** idempotently (check if command already exists)
   556→5. **Marshal modified types back** to the raw map
   557→6. **Write the file** with pretty-printing
   558→
   559→Key principles:
   560→- **Preserve unknown fields** - don't destroy user's custom hooks or settings
   561→- **Idempotent installs** - running `entire enable` twice doesn't duplicate hooks
   562→- **Support `localDev` mode** - use `go run ${PROJECT_DIR}/...` for development
   563→- **Identify Entire hooks** by command prefix (e.g., `"entire "` or `"go run ${...}"`)
   564→
   565→### Example: Claude Code Hook Config
   566→
   567→```json
   568→{
   569→  "hooks": {
   570→    "SessionStart": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks claude-code session-start"}]}],
   571→    "Stop": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks claude-code stop"}]}],
   572→    "PreToolUse": [{"matcher": "Task", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks claude-code pre-task"}]}]
   573→  }
   574→}
   575→```
   576→
   577→### Example: Gemini CLI Hook Config
   578→
   579→```json
   580→{
   581→  "hooksConfig": {"enabled": true},
   582→  "hooks": {
   583→    "SessionStart": [{"hooks": [{"name": "entire-session-start", "type": "command", "command": "entire hooks gemini session-start"}]}],
   584→    "AfterAgent": [{"hooks": [{"name": "entire-after-agent", "type": "command", "command": "entire hooks gemini after-agent"}]}]
   585→  }
   586→}
   587→```
   588→
   589→Note: Gemini CLI requires `hooksConfig.enabled: true` and each hook entry requires a `name` field.
   590→
   591→## Testing Patterns
   592→
   593→### Testing `ParseHookEvent`
   594→
   595→Test every hook name, including pass-through hooks that return nil:
   596→
   597→```go
   598→func TestParseHookEvent_TurnStart(t *testing.T) {
   599→    t.Parallel()
   600→
   601→    ag := &YourAgent{}
   602→    input := `{"session_id": "sess-123", "transcript_path": "/tmp/t.jsonl", "prompt": "Fix the bug"}`
   603→
   604→    event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(HookNamePromptSubmit, strings.NewReader(input))
   605→
   606→    if err != nil {
   607→        t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   608→    }
   609→    if event == nil {
   610→        t.Fatal("expected event, got nil")
   611→    }
   612→    if event.Type != agent.TurnStart {
   613→        t.Errorf("expected TurnStart, got %v", event.Type)
   614→    }
   615→    if event.Prompt != "Fix the bug" {
   616→        t.Errorf("expected prompt 'Fix the bug', got %q", event.Prompt)
   617→    }
   618→}
   619→```
   620→
   621→### Testing Nil Returns
   622→
   623→Hooks with no lifecycle action must return `nil, nil`:
   624→
   625→```go
   626→func TestParseHookEvent_PassThrough_ReturnsNil(t *testing.T) {
   627→    t.Parallel()
   628→
   629→    ag := &YourAgent{}
   630→    event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent("some-pass-through-hook", strings.NewReader(`{}`))
   631→
   632→    if err != nil {
   633→        t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   634→    }
   635→    if event != nil {
   636→        t.Errorf("expected nil event, got %+v", event)
   637→    }
   638→}
   639→```
   640→

   707→### Test file references
   708→
   709→- Claude Code lifecycle tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/lifecycle_test.go`
   710→- Claude Code hooks tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/hooks_test.go`
   711→- Claude Code transcript tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/transcript_test.go`
   712→- Gemini CLI lifecycle tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/lifecycle_test.go`
   713→- Gemini CLI hooks tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/hooks_test.go`
   714→- Gemini CLI transcript tests: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript_test.go`
   715→
   716→## Common Pitfalls
   717→
   718→### go-git v5 Bugs
   719→
   720→**Do NOT use go-git v5 for `checkout` or `reset --hard` operations.** go-git v5 has a bug where `worktree.Reset()` with `HardReset` and `worktree.Checkout()` incorrectly delete untracked directories even when listed in `.gitignore`. This would destroy `.entire/` and agent config directories. Use the git CLI instead. See `CLAUDE.md` for details and `hard_reset_test.go` for regression tests.
   721→
   722→### Repo Root vs Current Working Directory
   723→
   724→Git commands return paths relative to the **repository root**, not the current working directory. When your code runs from a subdirectory, `os.Getwd()` gives wrong results for path construction. Always use `paths.RepoRoot()`:
   725→
   726→```go
   727→// WRONG
   728→cwd, _ := os.Getwd()
   729→absPath := filepath.Join(cwd, file) // Breaks from subdirectory
   730→
   731→// CORRECT
   732→repoRoot, _ := paths.RepoRoot()
   733→absPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, file)
   734→```
   735→
   736→### Transcript Flush Timing
   737→
   738→Some agents write transcripts asynchronously. If `ReadTranscript` is called before the write completes, the transcript will be incomplete. Implement `TranscriptPreparer` if your agent has this behavior. Claude Code solves this by writing a sentinel entry and polling for it (see `waitForTranscriptFlush` in `claudecode/lifecycle.go`).
   739→
   740→### Nil Event Return Pattern
   741→
   742→`ParseHookEvent` returning `(nil, nil)` is **not an error** - it means the hook has no lifecycle significance. The framework (in `hook_registry.go`) checks:
   743→
   744→```go
   745→event, parseErr := ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, stdin)
   746→if event != nil {
   747→    hookErr = DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)
   748→}
   749→// nil event → no-op (hook is silently acknowledged)
   750→```
   751→
   752→Use `//nolint:nilnil` to suppress the linter warning on intentional nil returns.
   753→
   754→### Agent Name vs Agent Type
   755→
   756→- `AgentName` is the **registry key** used in code (`"claude-code"`, `"gemini"`). It appears in CLI commands: `entire hooks claude-code stop`.
   757→- `AgentType` is the **display name** stored in metadata and commit trailers (`"Claude Code"`, `"Gemini CLI"`). It's what users see.
   758→
   759→Register constants for both in `cmd/entire/cli/agent/registry.go` when adding a new agent.
   760→
   761→### Hook Names as CLI Subcommands
   762→
   763→The strings returned by `HookNames()` become literal CLI subcommands under `entire hooks <agent>`. For example, if `HookNames()` returns `["session-start", "stop"]`, the CLI creates:
   764→- `entire hooks your-agent session-start`
   765→- `entire hooks your-agent stop`
   766→
   767→These commands read JSON from stdin and dispatch to `ParseHookEvent`. The agent's hook config should invoke these commands.
   768→
   769→## Complete Code Template
   770→
   771→A minimal but functional agent skeleton. Copy this directory structure and fill in agent-specific details:
   772→

   777→package youragent
   778→
   779→// sessionInfoRaw matches your agent's session hook JSON payload.
   780→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
   781→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   782→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
   783→}
   784→
   785→// promptInputRaw matches your agent's prompt-submit hook JSON payload.
   786→type promptInputRaw struct {
   787→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   788→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
   789→	Prompt         string `json:"prompt"`
   790→}
   791→```
