   799→package youragent
   800→
   801→import (
   802→	"errors"
   803→	"fmt"
   804→	"io"
   805→	"os"
   806→	"path/filepath"
   807→
   808→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   809→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
   810→)
   811→
   812→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
   813→func init() {
   814→	agent.Register("your-agent", NewYourAgent)
   815→}
   816→
   817→type YourAgent struct{}
   818→
   819→func NewYourAgent() agent.Agent { return &YourAgent{} }
   820→
   821→func (a *YourAgent) Name() agent.AgentName  { return "your-agent" }
   822→func (a *YourAgent) Type() agent.AgentType   { return "Your Agent" }
   823→func (a *YourAgent) Description() string     { return "Your Agent - brief description" }
   824→func (a *YourAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".youragent"} }
   825→
   826→func (a *YourAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
   827→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   828→	if err != nil {
   829→		repoRoot = "."
   830→	}
   831→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".youragent")); err == nil {
   832→		return true, nil
   833→	}
   834→	return false, nil
   835→}
   836→
   837→func (a *YourAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
   838→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path from agent hook
   839→	if err != nil {
   840→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   841→	}
   842→	return data, nil
   843→}
   844→
   845→func (a *YourAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   846→	return agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   847→}
   848→
   849→func (a *YourAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   850→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   851→}
   852→
   853→// --- Session Management ---
   854→func (a *YourAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string                                          { return "" }
   855→func (a *YourAgent) SupportsHooks() bool                                                { return true }
   856→func (a *YourAgent) ParseHookInput(_ agent.HookType, _ io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) { return nil, errors.New("use ParseHookEvent") }
   857→func (a *YourAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string                         { return input.SessionID }
   858→func (a *YourAgent) GetSessionDir(_ string) (string, error)                             { return "", errors.New("not implemented") }
   859→func (a *YourAgent) ResolveSessionFile(dir, id string) string                           { return filepath.Join(dir, id+".jsonl") }
   860→func (a *YourAgent) ReadSession(_ *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error)        { return nil, errors.New("not implemented") }
   861→func (a *YourAgent) WriteSession(_ *agent.AgentSession) error                           { return errors.New("not implemented") }
   862→func (a *YourAgent) FormatResumeCommand(id string) string                               { return "youragent --resume " + id }

   867→<details>
   868→<summary>youragent/lifecycle.go</summary>
   869→
   870→```go
   871→package youragent
   872→
   873→import (
   874→	"io"
   875→	"time"
   876→
   877→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   878→)
   879→
   880→const (
   881→	HookNameSessionStart = "session-start"
   882→	HookNameSessionEnd   = "session-end"
   883→	HookNamePromptSubmit = "prompt-submit"
   884→	HookNameResponse     = "response"
   885→)
   886→
   887→func (a *YourAgent) HookNames() []string {
   888→	return []string{
   889→		HookNameSessionStart,
   890→		HookNameSessionEnd,
   891→		HookNamePromptSubmit,
   892→		HookNameResponse,
   893→	}
   894→}
   895→
   896→func (a *YourAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   897→	switch hookName {
   898→	case HookNameSessionStart:
   899→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   900→		if err != nil {
   901→			return nil, err
   902→		}
   903→		return &agent.Event{
   904→			Type:       agent.SessionStart,
   905→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   906→			SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   907→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   908→		}, nil
   909→
   910→	case HookNamePromptSubmit:
   911→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[promptInputRaw](stdin)
   912→		if err != nil {
   913→			return nil, err
   914→		}
   915→		return &agent.Event{
   916→			Type:       agent.TurnStart,
   917→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   918→			SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   919→			Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
   920→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   921→		}, nil
   922→
   923→	case HookNameResponse:
   924→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   925→		if err != nil {
   926→			return nil, err
   927→		}
   928→		return &agent.Event{
   929→			Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   930→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   931→			SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   932→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   933→		}, nil
   934→
   935→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
   936→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   937→		if err != nil {
   938→			return nil, err
   939→		}
   940→		return &agent.Event{
   941→			Type:       agent.SessionEnd,
   942→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   943→			SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   944→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   945→		}, nil
   946→
   947→	default:
   948→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
   949→	}
   950→}
   951→```
   952→
   953→</details>

 Key decisions in `ParseHookEvent`:
 
 - **Return `nil, nil`** for hooks with no lifecycle significance (pass-through hooks). This is not an error - it tells the framework to do nothing.
-- **Every event must have `SessionID` and `SessionRef`**. The framework validates these.
+- **Every event must include the required fields for its `EventType`** (see the [Event Field Requirements](#event-field-requirements) table). `SessionID` is always required; `SessionRef` is required for most event types but optional for `Compaction` and `SessionEnd`.
 - **`TurnStart` should include `Prompt`** if available - it's used for commit message generation.

> AGENT

All 6 PR comments addressed:

1. **SessionRef requirements** — Changed blanket "every event must have SessionID and SessionRef" to point to the per-EventType requirements table
2. **Unused `encoding/json` import** — Removed
3. **Unused `time` import** — Removed
4. **`${PROJECT_DIR}` placeholder** — Changed to `${YOUR_AGENT_PROJECT_DIR}` with a comment explaining the pattern (each agent uses its own env var like `${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}`, `${GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR}`) and noting the need to add it to `entireHookPrefixes`
5. **`HookHandler` described as optional** — Updated to "**Required for CLI hook registration**" in the table and in Step 8's prose
6. **`ParseHookInput` stub** — Replaced error-returning stub with a real implementation using `ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw]` that populates `SessionID` and `SessionRef`, with a comment noting it's called by integration tests
